4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{0}\sim p_{T},

(B.1)

and its approximate version (
2.5
) with the learned score function

{\mathrm{d}}{\bm{y}}_{t}=\left[\dfrac{1}{2}{\bm{y}}_{t}+{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t}({\bm{y}}_{t})\right]{\mathrm{d}}t+{\mathrm{d}}{\bm{w}}_{t},\quad\text{with}\quad{\bm{y}}_{0}\sim{\mathcal{N}}(0,{\bm{I}}_{d}).

The filtration
{\mathcal{F}}_{t}
refers to the filtration of the SDE (
B.1
) up to time
t
.

B.1
Auxiliary Process

We would like first to consider the errors that Algorithm
1
may cause within one block of update. To this end, we consider the following auxiliary process for
\tau\in[0,h_{n}]
conditioned on the filtration
{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}
at time
t_{n}
:

Definition B.1

(Auxiliary Process)
.

For any
n\in[0:N-1]
, we define the auxiliary process
(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k)})_{\tau\in[0,h_{n}]}
as the solution to the following SDE recursively for
k\in[0:K-1]
:

{\mathrm{d}}\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k+1)}(\omega)=\Bigg[\dfrac{1}{2}\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k+1)}(\omega)+{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}\Big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(k)}(\omega)\Big)\Bigg]{\mathrm{d}}\tau+{\mathrm{d}}{\bm{w}}_{t_{n}+\tau}(\omega),

(B.2)

with the initial condition

\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(0)}(\omega)\equiv\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n}}(\omega)\ \text{for}\ \tau\in[0,h_{n}],\quad\text{and}\quad\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},0}^{(k)}(\omega)\equiv\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n}}(\omega)\ \text{for}\ k\in[1:K]

(B.3)

where
\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n}}(\omega)=\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n-1},\tau_{n-1,M_{n-1}}}^{(K)}(\omega)
if
n\in[1:N-1]
and
\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{0}}(\omega)\sim{\mathcal{N}}(0,{\bm{I}}_{d})
.

The iteration should be perceived as a deterministic procedure to each event
\omega\in\Omega
,
i.e.
each realization of the Wiener process
({\bm{w}}_{t})_{t\geq 0}
. The following lemma clarifies this fact and proves the well-definedness and parallelability of the iteration in (
B.2
).

Lemma B.2
.

The auxiliary process
(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k)}(\omega))_{\tau\in[0,h_{n}]}
is
{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}+\tau}
-adapted for any
k\in[0:K]
and
n\in[0:N-1]
.

Proof.

Since the initialization
\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(0)}(\omega)\equiv\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n}}(\omega)
for
\tau\in[0,h_{n}]
, where
\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n}}(\omega)
is
{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}
-adapted, it is obvious that
\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(0)}(\omega)
is
{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}+\tau}
-adapted. Now suppose that
(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k)}(\omega))_{\tau\in[0,h_{n}]}
is
{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}+\tau}
-adapted, since
g_{n}(\tau)\leq\tau
, we have the following Itô integral well-defined and
{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}+\tau}
-adapted:

\int_{0}^{\tau}{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}\Big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}^{(k)}(\omega)\Big){\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime},

and therefore (
B.2
) has a unique strong solution
(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k+1)}(\omega))_{\tau\in[0,h_{n}]}
that is also
{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}+\tau}
-adapted. The lemma follows by induction.
∎

Lemma B.3

(Equivalence between (
3.4
) and (
B.2
))
.

For any
n\in[0:N-1]
, the update rule (
3.4
) in Algorithm
1
is equivalent to the exact solution of the auxiliary process (
B.2
) for any
k\in[0:K-1]
and
\tau\in[0,h_{n}]
.

Proof.

The dependency on
\omega
will be omitted in the proof below.

Rewriting (
B.2
) and multiplying
e^{-\frac{\tau}{2}}
on both sides yield

\displaystyle{\mathrm{d}}\left[e^{-\frac{\tau}{2}}\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k+1)}\right]

\displaystyle=e^{-\frac{\tau}{2}}\left[{\mathrm{d}}\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k+1)}-\frac{1}{2}\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k+1)}{\mathrm{d}}\tau\right]=e^{-\frac{\tau}{2}}\left[{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}\Big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(k)}\Big){\mathrm{d}}\tau+{\mathrm{d}}{\bm{w}}_{t_{n}+\tau}\right].

Integrating on both sides from
0
to
\tau
implies

\displaystyle e^{-\frac{\tau}{2}}\widehat{\bm{y}}^{(k+1)}_{t_{n},\tau}-\widehat{\bm{y}}^{(k+1)}_{t_{n},0}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\tau}e^{-\frac{\tau^{\prime}}{2}}\Bigg({\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}\Big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}^{(k)}\Big){\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}+{\mathrm{d}}{\bm{w}}_{t_{n}+\tau^{\prime}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=\sum_{m=0}^{M_{n}}\int_{\tau\wedge t_{n,m}}^{\tau\wedge\tau_{n,m+1}}e^{-\frac{\tau^{\prime}}{2}}{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+\tau_{n,m}}\Big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau_{n,m}}^{(k)}\Big){\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{\tau}e^{-\frac{\tau^{\prime}}{2}}{\mathrm{d}}{\bm{w}}_{t_{n}+\tau^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{m=0}^{M_{n}}2\left(e^{-\frac{\tau\wedge\tau_{n,m}}{2}}-e^{-\frac{\tau\wedge\tau_{n,m+1}}{2}}\right){\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+\tau_{n,j}}\Big(\widehat{\bm{y}}^{(k)}_{t_{n},\tau_{n,m}}\Big)+\int_{0}^{\tau}e^{-\frac{\tau^{\prime}}{2}}{\mathrm{d}}{\bm{w}}_{t_{n}+\tau^{\prime}},

and then multiplying
e^{\frac{\tau}{2}}
on both sides above yields

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{y}}^{(k+1)}_{t_{n},\tau}

\displaystyle=e^{\frac{\tau}{2}}\widehat{\bm{y}}^{(k+1)}_{t_{n},0}+\sum_{m=0}^{M_{n}}2\left(e^{\frac{\tau\wedge\tau_{n,m+1}-\tau\wedge\tau_{n,m}}{2}}-1\right)e^{\frac{0\vee(\tau-\tau_{n,m+1})}{2}}{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+\tau_{n,m}}\Big(\widehat{\bm{y}}^{(k)}_{t_{n},\tau_{n,m}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\sum_{m=0}^{M_{n}}\int_{\tau\wedge\tau_{n,m}}^{\tau\wedge\tau_{n,m+1}}e^{\frac{\tau-\tau^{\prime}}{2}}{\mathrm{d}}{\bm{w}}_{t_{n}+\tau^{\prime}},

where, by Itô isometry, we have

\int_{\tau\wedge\tau_{n,m}}^{\tau\wedge\tau_{n,m+1}}e^{\frac{\tau-\tau^{\prime}}{2}}{\mathrm{d}}{\bm{w}}_{t_{n}+\tau^{\prime}}\sim{\mathcal{N}}\left({\bm{0}},\left(e^{\tau\wedge\tau_{n,m+1}-\tau\wedge\tau_{n,m}}-1\right)e^{0\vee(\tau-\tau_{n,m+1})}{\bm{I}}_{d}\right)

for
\tau>\tau_{n,m}
and equals to
{\bm{0}}
otherwise. Plugging in
\tau=\tau_{j,m}
gives us (
3.4
), as desired.
∎

B.2
Errors within Block

We shall invoke Girsanov’s theorem (Theorem
A.4
) in the procedure as detailed below:

1.

Setting (
A.2
) in Theorem
A.4
as the auxiliary process (
B.2
) at iteration
K
, where
{\bm{w}}_{t}(\omega)
is a Wiener process under the measure
q|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}
;

2.

Defining another process
\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{t_{n}+\tau}(\omega)
governed by the following SDE:

{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{t_{n}+\tau}(\omega)={\mathrm{d}}{\bm{w}}_{t_{n}+\tau}(\omega)+{\bm{\delta}}_{t_{n}}(\tau,\omega)d\tau,

where

{\bm{\delta}}_{t_{n}}(\tau,\omega):={\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(K-1)}(\omega))-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n}+\tau}^{(K)}(\omega)),

(B.4)

and computing the Radon-Nikodym derivative of the measure
{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}
with respect to
q|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}
as

\dfrac{{\mathrm{d}}{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}{{\mathrm{d}}q|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}(\omega):=\exp\left(-\int_{0}^{h_{n}}{\bm{\delta}}_{t_{n}}(\tau,\omega)^{\top}{\mathrm{d}}{\bm{w}}_{t_{n}+\tau}(\omega)-\dfrac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{h_{n}}\|{\bm{\delta}}_{t_{n}}(\tau,\omega)\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau\right),

3.

Concluding that (
B.2
) at iteration
K
under the measure
q|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}
satisfies the following SDE:

{\mathrm{d}}\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(K)}(\omega)=\left[\dfrac{1}{2}\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(K)}(\omega)+\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}\big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(K)}(\omega)\big)\right]{\mathrm{d}}\tau+{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{t_{n}+\tau}(\omega),

with
(\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{t_{n}+\tau})_{\tau\geq 0}
being a Wiener process under the measure
{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}
. If we replace
\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(K)}(\omega)
by
{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}(\omega)
, one should notice (
B.5
) is immediately the original backward SDE (
2.3
) with the true score function on
t\in[t_{n},t_{n+1}]
:

{\mathrm{d}}{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}(\omega)=\left[\dfrac{1}{2}{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}(\omega)+\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}(\omega))\right]{\mathrm{d}}\tau+{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{t_{n}+\tau}(\omega).

(B.5)

Remark B.4
.

The applicability of Girsanov’s theorem here relies on the
{\mathcal{F}}_{\tau}
-adaptivity of
{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}\Big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(K-1)}(\omega)\big)
established by Lemma
B.2
. One should notice the change of measure procedure above depends on the number of iterations
K
, and different
K
would lead to different transform (
B.4
).

Then Corollary
A.5
provides the following computation

\displaystyle D_{\mathrm{KL}}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}}\|\widehat{q}_{t_{n+1}})

\displaystyle\leq D_{\mathrm{KL}}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}:t_{n+1}}\|\widehat{q}_{t_{n}:t_{n+1}})

(B.6)

\displaystyle=D_{\mathrm{KL}}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}}\|\widehat{q}_{t_{n}})+\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim q|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\dfrac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{h_{n}}\|{\bm{\delta}}_{t_{n}}(\tau,\omega)\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau\right],

where the first inequality is by the data-processing inequality (Theorem
A.1
). Now, the problem remaining is to bound the discrepancy quantified by

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{h_{n}}\|{\bm{\delta}}_{t_{n}}(\tau,\omega)\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau

(B.7)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{h_{n}}\left\|{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(K-1)}(\omega))-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(K)}(\omega))\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\Bigg(\underbrace{\int_{0}^{h_{n}}\left\|\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}\big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(K)}(\omega)\big)-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}\big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(K)}(\omega)\big)\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau}_{:=A_{t_{n}}(\omega)}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\int_{0}^{h_{n}}\left\|{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}\big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(K)}(\omega)\big)-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}\big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(K)}(\omega)\big)\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau}_{:=B_{t_{n}}(\omega)}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{h_{n}}\left\|{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}\big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(K)}(\omega)\big)-{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}\big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(K-1)}(\omega)\big)\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau\Bigg).

Before we continue our proof, we would like first to provide the following lemma bounding the behavior of the auxiliary process (
B.2
) when
k=0
for
\tau\in[0,h_{n}]
.

Lemma B.5
.

For any
n\in[0:N-1]
, suppose the initialization
\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n}}
in (
B.3
) of the auxiliary process (
B.2
) follows the distribution of
{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}}\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}}
, then the following estimate holds

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in[0,h_{n}]}\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(1)}(\omega)-\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(0)}(\omega)\|^{2}\right]

(B.8)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle h_{n}e^{\frac{7}{2}h_{n}}\left(M_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}+2d\right)+3e^{\frac{7}{2}h_{n}}\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[A_{t_{n}}(\omega)+B_{t_{n}}(\omega)\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 3e^{\frac{7}{2}h_{n}}h_{n}L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}\sup_{\tau\in[0,h_{n}]}\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(K)}(\omega)-\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(K-1)}(\omega)\right\|^{2}\right].

Proof.

Let
{\bm{z}}_{t_{n},\tau}=\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(1)}-\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(0)}
. For
k=0
, we can rewrite (
B.2
) as

{\mathrm{d}}{\bm{z}}_{t_{n},\tau}=\Bigg[\dfrac{1}{2}\left({\bm{z}}_{t_{n},\tau}+\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(0)}\right)+{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}\Big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(0)}\Big)\Bigg]{\mathrm{d}}\tau+{\mathrm{d}}{\bm{w}}_{t_{n}+\tau},

By applying Itô’s lemma and plugging in the expression of
{\bm{w}}_{t_{n}+\tau}
given by Theorem
A.4
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathrm{d}}\|{\bm{z}}_{t_{n},\tau}\|^{2}=

\displaystyle\Bigg[\|{\bm{z}}_{t_{n},\tau}\|^{2}+{\bm{z}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{\top}\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(0)}+2{\bm{z}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{\top}{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}\Big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(0)}\Big)+d\Bigg]{\mathrm{d}}\tau

(B.9)

\displaystyle+2{\bm{z}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{\top}\big({\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{t_{n}+\tau}(\omega)-{\bm{\delta}}_{t_{n}}(\tau,\omega)d\tau\big),

By integrating from
0
to
\tau
and taking the expectation on both sides of (
B.9
), we obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\|{\bm{z}}_{t_{n},\tau}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\int_{0}^{\tau}\Bigg(\|{\bm{z}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}\|^{2}+{\bm{z}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{\top}\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{(0)}+2{\bm{z}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{\top}{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}\Big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}^{(0)}\Big)+d\Bigg){\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 2\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\int_{0}^{\tau}{\bm{z}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{\top}\Big({\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{t_{n}+\tau^{\prime}}(\omega)-{\bm{\delta}}_{t_{n}}(\tau^{\prime},\omega){\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}\Big)\right],

and by AM-GM, we further have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\|{\bm{z}}_{t_{n},\tau}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\int_{0}^{\tau}\Bigg[\dfrac{7}{2}\|{\bm{z}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}\|^{2}+\dfrac{1}{2}\left\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{(0)}\right\|^{2}+\left\|{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}\Big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}^{(0)}\Big)\right\|^{2}+d+\|{\bm{\delta}}_{t_{n}}(\tau,\omega)\|^{2}\Bigg]{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\tau}\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\dfrac{7}{2}\|{\bm{z}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}\|^{2}+\|{\bm{\delta}}_{t_{n}}(\tau,\omega)\|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}+\left(\dfrac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(0)}\right\|^{2}\right]+M_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}+d\right)\tau,

where
{\bm{\delta}}_{t_{n}}(\tau,\omega)
is defined in (
B.4
). Similar to (
B.7
), we may use triangle inequality to upper bound
\|{\bm{\delta}}_{t_{n}}(\tau,\omega)\|^{2}
, which implies that for any
\tau\in[0,h_{n}]

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\|{\bm{z}}_{t_{n},\tau}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{7}{2}\int_{0}^{\tau}\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\|{\bm{z}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}\|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}+\left(\dfrac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(0)}\right\|^{2}\right]+M_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}+d\right)\tau

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 3\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\int_{0}^{\tau}\left\|{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(K-1)}(\omega))-{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(K)}(\omega))\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 3\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\int_{0}^{\tau}\left\|{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(K)}(\omega))-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(K)}(\omega))\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 3\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\int_{0}^{\tau}\left\|\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(K)}(\omega))-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n}+\tau}^{(K)}(\omega))\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{7}{2}\int_{0}^{\tau}\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\|{\bm{z}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}\|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}+\left(\dfrac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(0)}\right\|^{2}\right]+M_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}+d\right)\tau

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 3L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}\int_{0}^{\tau}\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}^{(K)}(\omega)-\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}^{(K-1)}(\omega)\right\|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}+3\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[A_{t_{n}}(\omega)+B_{t_{n}}(\omega)\right],

where in the second inequality above, we have used the fact that
s^{\theta}_{t}(\cdot)
is
L_{{\bm{s}}}
-Lipschitz for any
t
. By Grönwall’s inequality, we have that for any
\tau\in[0,h_{n}]

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\|{\bm{z}}_{t_{n},\tau}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq e^{\frac{7}{2}\tau}\left[\left(\dfrac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(0)}\right\|^{2}\right]+M_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}+d\right)\tau\right]

(B.10)

\displaystyle+3e^{\frac{7}{2}\tau}\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[A_{t_{n}}(\omega)+B_{t_{n}}(\omega)\right]

\displaystyle+3e^{\frac{7}{2}\tau}L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}\int_{0}^{\tau}\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}^{(K)}(\omega)-\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}^{(K-1)}(\omega)\right\|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}.

By assumption,
\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(0)}=\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n}}
follows the distribution of
{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}}\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}}
, which allows us to bound the second moment of
\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n}}
for any
n\in[0:N]
by Lemma
A.6
:

\mathbb{E}\left[\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n}}\|^{2}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}}\|^{2}\right]\leq 2d.

Substituting (
A.5
) into (
B.10
) then yields that for any
\tau\in[0,h_{n}]

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim q|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\|{\bm{z}}_{t_{n},\tau}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\tau e^{\frac{7}{2}\tau}\left(M_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}+2d\right)+3e^{\frac{7}{2}\tau}\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[A_{t_{n}}(\omega)+B_{t_{n}}(\omega)\right]

\displaystyle+3\tau e^{\frac{7}{2}\tau}L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}\sup_{\tau^{\prime}\in[0,h_{n}]}\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{(K)}(\omega)-\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{(K-1)}(\omega)\right\|^{2}\right].

Taking supremum with respect to
\tau\in[0,h_{n}]
on both sides above completes our proof.
∎

As utilized in the proof of the existence of solutions of SDEs, the following lemma demonstrates the exponential convergence of the iteration defined in (
B.2
).

Lemma B.6

(Exponential convergence of Picard iteration in PIADM-SDE)
.

For any
n\in[0,N]
, suppose the initialization
\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n}}
in (
B.3
) of the auxiliary process (
B.2
) follows the distribution of
{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}}\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}}
, then the two ending terms
\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(K)}
and
\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(K-1)}
of the sequence
\{\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k)}\}_{k\in[0:K-1]}
satisfy the following exponential convergence rate

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in[0,h_{n}]}\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(K)}(\omega)-\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(K-1)}(\omega)\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{\left(L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}h_{n}e^{2h_{n}}\right)^{K-1}he^{\frac{7}{2}h_{n}}\left(M_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}+2d\right)}{1-3\left(L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}h_{n}e^{2h_{n}}\right)^{K-1}e^{\frac{7}{2}h_{n}}h_{n}L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}}+\dfrac{3\left(L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}h_{n}e^{2h_{n}}\right)^{K-1}e^{\frac{7}{2}h_{n}}\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[A_{t_{n}}(\omega)+B_{t_{n}}(\omega)\right]}{1-3\left(L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}h_{n}e^{2h_{n}}\right)^{K-1}e^{\frac{7}{2}h_{n}}h_{n}L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}}.

Proof.

For each
\omega\in\Omega
conditioned on the filtration
{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}
, subtracting (
B.2
) from the process as defined by

{\mathrm{d}}\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k)}(\omega)=\left[\dfrac{1}{2}\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k)}(\omega)+{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}\Big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(k-1)}(\omega)\Big)\right]{\mathrm{d}}\tau+{\mathrm{d}}{\bm{w}}_{t_{n}+\tau}(\omega),

(B.11)

we have

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\mathrm{d}}\left(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k+1)}(\omega)-\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k)}(\omega)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left[\dfrac{1}{2}\left(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k+1)}(\omega)-\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k)}(\omega)\right)+{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}\big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(k)}(\omega)\big)-{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}\big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(k-1)}(\omega)\big)\right]{\mathrm{d}}\tau,

where the diffusion term
{\mathrm{d}}{\bm{w}}_{t_{n}+\tau}
cancels each other out. Now we may use the formula above to compute derivative
\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}}\left\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{(k+1)}(\omega)-\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{(k)}(\omega)\right\|^{2}
explicitly, integrate it from
\tau^{\prime}=0
to
\tau
, and obtain the following inequality

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k+1)}(\omega)-\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k)}(\omega)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\tau}2\left(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{(k+1)}(\omega)-\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{(k)}(\omega)\right)^{\top}\left({\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}\big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}^{(k)}(\omega)\big)-{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}\big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}^{(k-1)}(\omega)\big)\right){\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\tau}\left\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{(k+1)}(\omega)-\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{(k)}(\omega)\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\int_{0}^{\tau}\left\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{(k+1)}(\omega)-\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{(k)}(\omega)\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\tau}\left\|{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}\big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}^{(k)}(\omega)\big)-{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}\big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}^{(k-1)}(\omega)\big)\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\int_{0}^{\tau}\left\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{(k+1)}(\omega)-\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{(k)}(\omega)\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}+L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}\int_{0}^{\tau}\left\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}^{(k)}(\omega)-\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}^{(k-1)}(\omega)\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}.

By Grönwall’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k+1)}(\omega)-\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k)}(\omega)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}e^{2\tau}\int_{0}^{\tau}\left\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}^{(k)}(\omega)-\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}^{(k-1)}(\omega)\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}.

(B.12)

Taking expectation on both sides above further implies that for any
\tau\in[0,h_{n}]
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k+1)}(\omega)-\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k)}(\omega)\right\|^{2}\right]

(B.13)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}e^{2\tau}\int_{0}^{\tau}\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}^{(k)}(\omega)-\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}^{(k-1)}(\omega)\right\|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}\tau e^{2\tau}\sup_{\tau^{\prime}\in[0,\tau]}\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{(k)}(\omega)-\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{(k-1)}(\omega)\right\|^{2}\right].

Furthermore, we take supremum over
\tau\in[0,h_{n}]
on both sides above and iterate (
B.12
) over
k\in\mathbb{N}
, which indicates

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in[0,h_{n}]}\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k+1)}(\omega)-\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k)}(\omega)\right\|^{2}\right]

(B.14)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}h_{n}e^{2h_{n}}\sup_{\tau\in[0,h_{n}]}\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k)}(\omega)-\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{(k-1)}(\omega)\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}h_{n}e^{2h_{n}}\right)^{k}\sup_{\tau\in[0,h_{n}]}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(1)}(\omega)-\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(0)}(\omega)\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}h_{n}e^{2h_{n}}\right)^{k}he^{\frac{7}{2}h_{n}}\left(M_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}+2d\right)+3\left(L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}h_{n}e^{2h_{n}}\right)^{k}e^{\frac{7}{2}h_{n}}\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[A_{t_{n}}(\omega)+B_{t_{n}}(\omega)\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 3\left(L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}h_{n}e^{2h_{n}}\right)^{k}e^{\frac{7}{2}h_{n}}h_{n}L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}\sup_{\tau\in[0,h_{n}]}\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(K)}(\omega)-\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(K-1)}(\omega)\right\|^{2}\right],

where the last inequality follows from Lemma
B.5
. By rearranging the inequality above, setting
k=K-1
and using the assumption that
L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}h_{n}e^{2h_{n}}\ll 1
, we obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in[0,h_{n}]}\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(K)}(\omega)-\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(K-1)}(\omega)\right\|^{2}\right]

(B.15)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{\left(L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}h_{n}e^{2h_{n}}\right)^{K-1}he^{\frac{7}{2}h_{n}}\left(M_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}+2d\right)+3\left(L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}h_{n}e^{2h_{n}}\right)^{K-1}e^{\frac{7}{2}h_{n}}\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[A_{t_{n}}(\omega)+B_{t_{n}}(\omega)\right]}{1-3\left(L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}h_{n}e^{2h_{n}}\right)^{K-1}e^{\frac{7}{2}h_{n}}h_{n}L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}},

as desired.
∎

The following lemma from
[
107
]
bounds the expectation of the term
A_{t_{n}}(\omega)
in (
B.7
):

Lemma B.7

(
[
107
, Section 3.1]
)
.

We have

\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[A_{t_{n}}(\omega)\right]\lesssim\epsilon dh_{n},\quad\text{for}\ n\in[0:N-2],\ \text{and}\ \mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[A_{t_{N-1}}(\omega)\right]\lesssim\epsilon d\log\eta^{-1},

where
\eta
is the parameter for early stopping.

Proof.

Notice that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[A_{t_{n}}(\omega)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\int_{0}^{h_{n}}\left\|\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}\big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(K)}(\omega)\big)-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}\big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(K)}(\omega)\big)\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\sum_{m=0}^{M_{n}}\int_{\tau_{n,m}}^{\tau_{n,m+1}}\left\|\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau_{n,m}}\big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau_{n,m}}^{(K)}(\omega)\big)-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}\big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(K)}(\omega)\big)\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau\right],

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{m=0}^{M_{n}}\int_{\tau_{n,m}}^{\tau_{n,m+1}}\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau_{n,m}}\big({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}(\omega)\big)-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}\big({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}(\omega)\big)\right\|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\tau,

where for the last equality, we use the fact that the process
\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(K)}(\omega)
follows the backward SDE with the true score function under the measure

\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu

\textstyle p

. In the following, we drop the superscript
\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}
of the expectation for simplicity.

By Lemma
A.6
and
A.7
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau_{n,m}}\big({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}(\omega)\big)-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}\big({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}(\omega)\big)\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\tau}\left(\dfrac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau_{n,m}}\big({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern \displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\tau}\left(\dfrac{1}{2}d{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle\sigma$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle\sigma$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle\sigma$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle\sigma$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern
