4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}\right]\right),

Now noticing that

{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle\sigma$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle\sigma$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle\sigma$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\sigma$\cr}}}}_{t}^{2}=\sigma_{T-t}^{2}\lesssim T-t,

we further have

\displaystyle\int_{\tau_{n,m}}^{\tau_{n,m+1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau_{n,m}}\big({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}(\omega)\big)-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}\big({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}(\omega)\big)\right\|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\tau_{n,m}}^{\tau_{n,m+1}}\int_{0}^{\tau^{\prime}}\dfrac{d}{(T-t_{n}-\tau_{n,m+1})^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}{\mathrm{d}}\tau+\dfrac{\epsilon_{n,m}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\tr{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau_{n,m}}\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\tr{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau_{n,m+1}}\right]\right)}{(T-t_{n}-\tau_{n,m})^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle d\dfrac{\epsilon_{n,m}^{2}}{(T-t_{n}-\tau_{n,m+1})^{2}}+\dfrac{\epsilon\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\tr{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau_{n,m}}\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\tr{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau_{n,m+1}}\right]\right)}{T-t_{n}-\tau_{n,m}},

and thus

\displaystyle\sum_{m=0}^{M_{n}}\int_{\tau_{n,m}}^{\tau_{n,m+1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau_{n,m}}\big({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}(\omega)\big)-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}\big({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}(\omega)\big)\right\|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle d\sum_{m=0}^{M_{n}}\dfrac{\epsilon_{n,m}^{2}}{(T-t_{n}-\tau_{n,m+1})^{2}}+\sum_{m=0}^{M_{n}}\dfrac{\epsilon}{T-t_{n}-\tau_{n,m}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\tr{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau_{n,m}}\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\tr{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau_{n,m+1}}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle d\epsilon^{2}M_{n}+\dfrac{\epsilon\mathbb{E}\left[\tr{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau_{n,0}}\right]}{T-t_{n}-\tau_{n,0}}+\sum_{m=0}^{M_{n}}\dfrac{\epsilon\epsilon_{n,m}\mathbb{E}\left[\tr{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{\Sigma}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau_{n,m}}\right]}{(T-t_{n}-\tau_{n,m+1})(T-t_{n}-\tau_{n,m})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle d\epsilon^{2}M_{n}+\epsilon d+d\epsilon^{2}M_{n}\lesssim d\epsilon^{2}M_{n}.

For
n\in[0,N-2]
, we have
M_{n}\epsilon=h_{n}
and thus
\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[A_{t_{n}}(\omega)\right]\lesssim\epsilon dh_{n}
, and for
n=N-1
, we have

M_{N}\lesssim\int_{\eta}^{h}\dfrac{1}{\epsilon\tau}{\mathrm{d}}\tau=\log\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{-1}

and thus
\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[A_{t_{N-1}}(\omega)\right]\lesssim\epsilon^{2}dM_{n}\lesssim\epsilon d\log\eta^{-1}
.
∎

B.3
Overall Error Bound

Proof of Theorem
3.3
.

We first continue the computation in (
B.6
) and (
B.7
):

\displaystyle D_{\mathrm{KL}}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}}\|\widehat{q}_{t_{n+1}})\leq D_{\mathrm{KL}}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}}\|\widehat{q}_{t_{n}})+\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\dfrac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{h_{n}}\|{\bm{\delta}}_{t_{n}}(\tau,\omega)\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle D_{\mathrm{KL}}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}}\|\widehat{q}_{t_{n}})+3\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[A_{t_{n}}(\omega)+B_{t_{n}}(\omega)\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 3\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\int_{0}^{h_{n}}\left\|{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}\big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(K)}(\omega)\big)-{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}\big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(K-1)}(\omega)\big)\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle D_{\mathrm{KL}}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}}\|\widehat{q}_{t_{n}})+3\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[A_{t_{n}}(\omega)+B_{t_{n}}(\omega)+L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}\int_{0}^{h_{n}}\left\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(K)}(\omega)-\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(K-1)}(\omega)\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle D_{\mathrm{KL}}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}}\|\widehat{q}_{t_{n}})+3\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[A_{t_{n}}(\omega)+B_{t_{n}}(\omega)+h_{n}L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}\sup_{\tau\in[0,h_{n}]}\left\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(K)}(\omega)-\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(K-1)}(\omega)\right\|^{2}\right].

Then plugging in the result of Lemma
B.6
, we have

\displaystyle D_{\mathrm{KL}}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}}\|\widehat{q}_{t_{n+1}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle D_{\mathrm{KL}}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}}\|\widehat{q}_{t_{n}})+3\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[A_{t_{n}}(\omega)+B_{t_{n}}(\omega)\right]+3h_{n}L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}\dfrac{\left(L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}h_{n}e^{2h_{n}}\right)^{K-1}he^{\frac{7}{2}h_{n}}\left(M_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}+2d\right)}{1-3\left(L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}h_{n}e^{2h_{n}}\right)^{K-1}e^{\frac{7}{2}h_{n}}h_{n}L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle h_{n}L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}\dfrac{9\left(L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}h_{n}e^{2h_{n}}\right)^{K-1}e^{\frac{7}{2}h_{n}}\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[A_{t_{n}}(\omega)+B_{t_{n}}(\omega)\right]}{1-3\left(L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}h_{n}e^{2h_{n}}\right)^{K-1}e^{\frac{7}{2}h_{n}}h_{n}L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle D_{\mathrm{KL}}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}}\|\widehat{q}_{t_{n}})+\dfrac{1+e^{-K}h_{n}e^{h_{n}}}{1-e^{-K}h_{n}e^{h_{n}}}\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[A_{t_{n}}(\omega)+B_{t_{n}}(\omega)\right]+e^{-K}h_{n}^{2}e^{h_{n}}d

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle D_{\mathrm{KL}}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}}\|\widehat{q}_{t_{n}})+\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[A_{t_{n}}(\omega)+B_{t_{n}}(\omega)\right]+e^{-K}h_{n}^{2}e^{h_{n}}d,

where we used the assumption that
L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}h_{n}e^{\frac{7}{2}h_{n}}\ll 1
.

The term
\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}[B_{t_{n}}(\omega)]
is bounded by Assumption
3.1
as

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}[B_{t_{n}}(\omega)]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\int_{0}^{h_{n}}\left\|{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}\big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(K)}(\omega)\big)-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}\big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(K)}(\omega)\big)\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\sum_{m=0}^{M_{n}-1}\epsilon_{n,m}\left\|{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+\tau_{n,m}}\big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau_{n,m}}^{(K)}(\omega)\big)-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau_{n,m}}\big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau_{n,m}}^{(K)}(\omega)\big)\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\sum_{m=0}^{M_{n}-1}\epsilon_{n,m}\left\|{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+\tau_{n,m}}\big({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}(\omega)\big)-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau_{n,m}}\big({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}(\omega)\big)\right\|^{2}\right]\leq\delta_{2}^{2},

where the last equality is because the process
\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(K)}(\omega)
under measure

\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu

\textstyle p

follows the backward SDE (
B.5
).

Thus, by Theorem
A.1
and plugging in the iteration relations above

\displaystyle D_{\mathrm{KL}}(p_{\eta}\|\widehat{q}_{t_{N}})=D_{\mathrm{KL}}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{N}}\|\widehat{q}_{t_{N}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle D_{\mathrm{KL}}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{0}\|\widehat{q}_{0})+\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}\left[A_{t_{n}}(\omega)+B_{t_{n}}(\omega)\right]+e^{-K}h_{n}^{2}e^{h_{n}}d\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle D_{\mathrm{KL}}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{0}\|\widehat{q}_{0})+\sum_{n=0}^{N-2}\epsilon dh_{n}+\epsilon d\log\eta^{-1}+\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\mathbb{E}_{\omega\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.10971pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-1.50694pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{t_{n}}}}[B_{t_{n}}(\omega)]+e^{-K}h_{n}^{2}e^{h_{n}}dN

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle de^{-T}+\epsilon d(T+\log\eta^{-1})+\delta_{2}^{2}+e^{-K}dT\leq de^{-T}+\epsilon dT+\delta^{2}+e^{-K}dT,

as
T\gtrsim\log\eta^{-1}
,
h_{n}\lesssim 1
, and
\delta_{2}\lesssim\delta
, and then it is straightforward to see that the following choices of parameters

\displaystyle T={\mathcal{O}}(\log(d\delta^{-2})),\quad h=\Theta(1),\quad N={\mathcal{O}}\left(\log(d\delta^{-2})\right),

\displaystyle\epsilon=\Theta\left(d^{-1}\delta^{2}\log^{-1}(d\delta^{-2})\right),\quad M={\mathcal{O}}\left(d\delta^{-2}\log(d\delta^{-2})\right),

\displaystyle K=\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\log(d\delta^{-2})),

would yield an overall error of
{\mathcal{O}}(\delta^{2})
.
∎

Appendix C
Details of Probability Flow ODE Implementation

In this section, we provide the details of the parallelized algorithm for the probability flow ODE formulation of diffusion models. We first introduce the algorithm and define the necessary notations, then discuss the error analysis during the predictor and corrector steps, respectively, and finally provide the proof of Theorem
3.5
.

C.1
Algorithm

Algorithm 2

PIADM-ODE

Input:

\widehat{\bm{y}}_{0}\sim\widehat{q}_{0}={\mathcal{N}}(0,{\bm{I}}_{d})
,
a discretization scheme (
T
,
(h_{n})_{n=1}^{N}
and

) satisfying (

,

,

),
the depth of iteration
K
and

, the learned NN-based score
{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t}(\cdot)
.

Output:

A sample
\widehat{\bm{y}}_{T}\sim\widehat{q}_{T}\approx{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{T}
.

In the parallelized inference algorithm for diffusion models in the probability flow ODE formulation, we adopt the same discretization scheme as in Section
3.1.1
and the exponential integrator for all updating rules. For each block, we first run a
predictor step
, which consists of running the probability flow ODE in parallel. Then we run a
corrector step
, which runs an underdamped Langevin dynamics in parallel to correct the distribution of the samples. The algorithm is summarized In Algorithm
2
.

Parallelized Predictor Step

The parallelization strategies in the predictor step are similar to those in the SDE algorithm (Algorithm
1
). The only difference here is that instead of applying Picard iteration to the backward SDE as in (
3.2
), we apply Picard iteration to the probability flow ODE as in (
C.3
), which does not require i.i.d. samples from standard Gaussian distribution. As shown in Lemma
C.3
, the update rule in the predictor step (
C.1
) in Algorithm
2
is equivalent to running the auxiliary predictor process (
C.3
). The auxiliary predictor process takes in the result from the previous corrector step (or the initialization if
n=0
) and outputs
\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},h_{n}}^{(K)}
as the initialization for the next corrector step.

Parallelized Corrector Step

The parallelization of the underdamped Langevin dynamics is similar to that mentioned in Section
2.2
. Given a sample resulting from the predictor step, we initialize the auxiliary corrector process (Definition
C.8
)
which is an underdamped Langevin dynamics with the initialization
\widehat{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},0}={\bm{y}}_{t_{n},h_{n}}^{(K)}
and the augmented variable
\widehat{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},0}\sim{\mathcal{N}}(0,{\bm{I}}_{d})
representing the momentum.

We run the underdamped Langevin dynamics for time
T^{\dagger}
, which is set to be of order
\Omega(1)
so that it is large enough to correct the distribution of the samples (
cf.
Lemma
C.18
) while being comparably short to ensure numerical stability (
cf.
Theorem
C.17
). Following a similar strategy as in Section
2.2
and in Algorithm
1
, we further divide the time horizon
T^{\dagger}
into
N^{\dagger}
blocks with step size
h^{\dagger}
, and for each block the block length
h^{\dagger}
into
M^{\dagger}
steps with step size
\epsilon^{\dagger}
. Within each block, we run the underdamped Langevin dynamics in parallel for
K^{\dagger}
iterations. As shown in Lemma
C.9
, the update rule in the corrector step (
C.2
) in Algorithm
2
is equivalent to running the auxiliary corrector process (
C.11
).

\widehat{q}_{t_{n}}=\widehat{q}_{t_{n},0}
\widehat{q}_{t_{n},h_{n}}=\widehat{\pi}_{t_{n},0}^{\widehat{\bm{u}}}
\widehat{\pi}_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}}^{\widehat{\bm{u}}}=\widehat{q}_{t_{n+1}}
\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k)}
\left(\widehat{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k)},\widehat{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k)}\right)
{\mathchoice{\vbox{\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}{\vbox{\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}{\vbox{\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}{\vbox{\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}_{t_{n}}
\widetilde{q}_{t_{n},h_{n}}=\pi_{t_{n},0}^{{\bm{u}}}=\widetilde{\pi}_{t_{n},0}^{\widetilde{\bm{u}}}
\widetilde{\pi}_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}}^{\widetilde{\bm{u}}}
\pi_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}}^{{\bm{u}}}
\widetilde{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k)}
\left(\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k)},\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k)}\right)
\left({\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}},{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}\right)
{\mathchoice{\vbox{\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}{\vbox{\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}{\vbox{\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}{\vbox{\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}_{t_{n+1}}=\pi_{t_{n},0}^{*,{\bm{u}}^{*}}
\pi_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}}^{*,{\bm{u}}^{*}}={\mathchoice{\vbox{\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}{\vbox{\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}{\vbox{\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}{\vbox{\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}_{t_{n+1}}
{\mathchoice{\vbox{\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}{\vbox{\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}{\vbox{\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}{\vbox{\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}
\left({\bm{u}}^{*}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}},{\bm{v}}^{*}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}\right)
W_{2}(\widetilde{q}_{t_{n},h_{n}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}{\vbox{\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}{\vbox{\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}{\vbox{\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}_{t_{n+1}})
(Theorem
C.7
)

D_{\mathrm{KL}}(\pi_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}}\|\widetilde{\pi}_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}})
(Theorem
C.17
)

\mathrm{TV}(\pi_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}{\vbox{\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}{\vbox{\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}{\vbox{\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}_{t_{n+1}})
(Lemma
C.18
)

\mathrm{TV}(\widehat{\pi}^{\widehat{\bm{u}}}_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}},\widetilde{\pi}^{\widetilde{{\bm{u}}}}_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}})
(Theorem
A.1
)

for PIADM-ODE within the

-th block.

In the following subsections, we proceed to provide theoretical guarantees for the algorithm.

C.2
Parallelized Predictor Step

Definition C.1

(Auxiliary Predictor Process)
.

For any
n\in[0:N-1]
, we define the auxiliary predictor process
(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k)})_{\tau\in[0,h_{n}]}
as the solution to the following ODE recursively for
k\in[0:K-1]
:

{\mathrm{d}}\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k+1)}=\Bigg[\dfrac{1}{2}\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k+1)}+\dfrac{1}{2}{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}\Big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(k)}\Big)\Bigg]{\mathrm{d}}\tau,

(C.3)

with the initial condition

\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(0)}\equiv\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n}}\ \text{for}\ \tau\in[0,h_{n}],\quad\text{and}\quad{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},0}^{(k)}\equiv\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n}}\ \text{for}\ k\in[1:K]

(C.4)

where
\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n}}=\widehat{\bm{u}}_{t_{n-1},N^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}
if
n\in[1:N-1]
and
\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{0}}\sim{\mathcal{N}}(0,{\bm{I}}_{d})
. We will also denote the probability distribution of
\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(K)}
as
\widehat{q}_{t_{n},\tau}
.

Definition C.2

(Interpolating Process)
.

For any
n\in[0:N-1]
, we define the interpolating process
(\widetilde{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k)})_{\tau\in[0,h_{n}]}
as the solution to the following ODE recursively for
k\in[0:K-1]
:

{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k+1)}=\Bigg[\dfrac{1}{2}\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k+1)}+\dfrac{1}{2}{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}\Big(\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(k)}\Big)\Bigg]{\mathrm{d}}\tau,

(C.5)

with initial condition

\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(0)}\equiv\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},0}^{(0)}\quad\text{for}\ \tau\in[0,h_{n}],\ \text{and}\ \widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},0}^{(k)}\equiv\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},0}^{(0)}\quad\text{for}\ k\in[1:K],

where
\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},0}^{(0)}\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}}
. We will also denote the probability distribution of
\widetilde{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(K)}
as
\widetilde{q}_{t_{n},\tau}
.

Similar to the equivalence between (
3.4
)
and (
B.2
), we have the following lemma:

Lemma C.3

(Equivalence between (
C.1
) and (
C.3
))
.

For any
n\in[0:N-1]
, the update rule (
C.1
) in Algorithm
2
is equivalent to the exact solution of (
C.3
)
for any
k\in[0:K-1]
and
\tau\in[0,h_{n}]
.

Proof.

Rewriting (
C.3
) and multiplying
e^{-\frac{\tau}{2}}
on both sides yield

{\mathrm{d}}\left[e^{-\frac{\tau}{2}}\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k+1)}\right]=e^{-\frac{\tau}{2}}\left[{\mathrm{d}}\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k+1)}-\frac{1}{2}\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k+1)}{\mathrm{d}}\tau\right]=\frac{e^{-\frac{\tau}{2}}}{2}{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}\Big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(k)}\Big){\mathrm{d}}\tau

Integrating on both sides from
0
to
\tau
implies

\displaystyle e^{-\frac{\tau}{2}}\widehat{\bm{y}}^{(k+1)}_{t_{n},\tau}-\widehat{\bm{y}}^{(k+1)}_{t_{n},0}=\int_{0}^{\tau}\frac{e^{-\frac{\tau}{2}}}{2}{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}\Big(\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}^{(k)}\Big){\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{2}\sum_{m=0}^{M_{n}}\int_{\tau\wedge t_{n,m}}^{\tau\wedge\tau_{n,m+1}}e^{-\frac{\tau^{\prime}}{2}}{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+\tau_{n,m}}\Big({\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau_{n,m}}^{(k)}\Big){\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{m=0}^{M_{n}}\left(e^{-\frac{\tau\wedge\tau_{n,m}}{2}}-e^{-\frac{\tau\wedge\tau_{n,m+1}}{2}}\right){\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+\tau_{n,j}}\Big(\widehat{\bm{y}}^{(k)}_{t_{n},\tau_{n,m}}\Big),

and then multiplying
e^{\frac{\tau}{2}}
on both sides above yields

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{y}}^{(k+1)}_{t_{n},\tau}=e^{\frac{\tau}{2}}\widehat{\bm{y}}^{(k+1)}_{t_{n},0}

\displaystyle+\sum_{m=0}^{M_{n}}\left(e^{\frac{\tau\wedge\tau_{n,m+1}-\tau\wedge\tau_{n,m}}{2}}-1\right)e^{\frac{0\vee(\tau-\tau_{n,m+1})}{2}}{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+\tau_{n,m}}\Big(\widehat{\bm{y}}^{(k)}_{t_{n},\tau_{n,m}}\Big).

Plugging in
\tau=\tau_{n,m}
gives us (
C.1
), as desired.
∎

Lemma C.4

(Error between the interpolating process and the true process)
.

Under the Picard iteration, we have that the ending process
\{\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(K)}\}_{\tau\in[0,h_{n}]}
satisfies the following exponential convergence rate

\sup_{\tau\in[0,h_{n}]}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(K)}-{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq 3d\left(\dfrac{h_{n}^{2}e^{h_{n}+\frac{3}{2}}L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}}{2}\right)^{K}+\frac{e^{h_{n}+\frac{3}{2}}h_{n}/2}{1-h_{n}^{2}e^{h_{n}+\frac{3}{2}}L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}/2}\Big(h_{n}\delta_{\infty}^{2}+\mathbb{E}[D_{t_{n}}]\Big),

where

D_{t_{n}}:=\int_{0}^{h_{n}}\left\|{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}\big({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}\big)-{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+\tau^{\prime}}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau^{\prime}})\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}.

Proof.

Recall that the backward true process
\{{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}\}_{\tau\in[0,h_{n}]}
satisfies the following backward SDE within one block

{\mathrm{d}}{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}=\left[\dfrac{1}{2}{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}+\dfrac{1}{2}\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau})\right]{\mathrm{d}}\tau.

(C.6)

By subtracting (
C.6
) from (
C.5
), we obtain that

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}\tau}\left(\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k+1)}-{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}\right)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{2}\left[\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k+1)}-{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}\right]

(C.7)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{2}\left[{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}\big(\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau)}^{(k)}\big)-{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)})\right]

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{2}\left[{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)})-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)})\right]

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{2}\left[\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau)})-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau})\right].

Then by

{\mathrm{d}}\left\|\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{(k+1)}-{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau^{\prime}}\right\|^{2}=2\left(\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{(k+1)}-{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau^{\prime}}\right)^{\top}{\mathrm{d}}\left(\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{(k+1)}-{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau^{\prime}}\right),

and integrating for
\tau^{\prime}\in[0,h_{n}]
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k+1)}-{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\tau}\left(\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{(k+1)}-{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau^{\prime}}\right)^{\top}\left({\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}\big(\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}^{(k)}\big)-{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})})\right){\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\tau}\left(\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{(k+1)}-{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})})\right){\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\tau}\left(\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{(k+1)}-{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern \displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\tau}\left\|\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{(k+1)}-{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau^{\prime}}\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}.

Using AM-GM inequality and taking expectations on both sides, we
further upper bound the summation above as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k+1)}-{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1+\dfrac{3}{2h_{n}}\right)\int_{0}^{\tau}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{(k+1)}-{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau^{\prime}}\right\|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\dfrac{h_{n}}{2}\int_{0}^{\tau}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}\big(\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}^{(k)}\big)-{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})})\right\|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\dfrac{h_{n}}{2}\int_{0}^{\tau}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\bm{s}}^{\theta}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})})-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})})\right\|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\dfrac{h_{n}}{2}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\underbrace{\int_{0}^{\tau}\left\|\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})})-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau^{\prime}})\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}}_{\leq D_{t_{n}}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1+\dfrac{3}{2h_{n}}\right)\int_{0}^{\tau}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{(k+1)}-{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau^{\prime}}\right\|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}+\dfrac{h_{n}}{2}\left(\tau\delta_{\infty}^{2}+\mathbb{E}\left[D_{t_{n}}\right]\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\dfrac{L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}h_{n}}{2}\int_{0}^{\tau}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}^{(k)}-{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}\right\|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime},

where the last equality is by Assumption
3.1’
.

Applying Grönwall’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k+1)}-{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}\right\|^{2}\right]

(C.8)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{e^{\left(1+\frac{3}{2h_{n}}\right)\tau}L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}h_{n}}{2}\int_{0}^{\tau}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}^{(k)}-{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}\right\|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}+\dfrac{e^{\left(1+\frac{3}{2h_{n}}\right)\tau}h_{n}}{2}\Big(\tau\delta_{\infty}^{2}+\mathbb{E}[D_{t_{n}}]\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{\tau e^{\left(1+\frac{3}{2h_{n}}\right)\tau}L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}h_{n}}{2}\sup_{\tau^{\prime}\in[0,\tau]}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{(k)}-{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau^{\prime}}\right\|^{2}\right]+\dfrac{e^{\left(1+\frac{3}{2h_{n}}\right)\tau}h_{n}}{2}\Big(\tau\delta_{\infty}^{2}+\mathbb{E}[D_{t_{n}}]\Big),

and by taking supremum

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in[0,h_{n}]}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(k+1)}-{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}\right\|^{2}\right]

(C.9)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{h_{n}^{2}e^{h_{n}+\frac{3}{2}}L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}}{2}\sup_{\tau^{\prime}\in[0,\tau]}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},\tau^{\prime}}^{(k)}-{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau^{\prime}}\right\|^{2}\right]+\dfrac{e^{h_{n}+\frac{3}{2}}h_{n}}{2}\Big(h_{n}\delta_{\infty}^{2}+\mathbb{E}[D_{t_{n}}]\Big)

Given that constant
h_{n}
is sufficiently small, which ensures
L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}h_{n}e^{\frac{5}{2}h_{n}}\ll 1
, iterating the above inequality for
k\in[0:K-1]
gives us that

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in[0,h_{n}]}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(K)}-{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\dfrac{h_{n}^{2}e^{h_{n}+\frac{3}{2}}L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}}{2}\right)^{K}\sup_{\tau\in[0,h_{n}]}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(0)}-{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}\right\|^{2}\right]+\frac{e^{h_{n}+\frac{3}{2}}h_{n}/2}{1-h_{n}^{2}e^{h_{n}+\frac{3}{2}}L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}/2}\Big(h_{n}\delta_{\infty}^{2}+\mathbb{E}[D_{t_{n}}]\Big),

Notice that by Lemma
A.6
, we have

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(0)}-{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}\right\|^{2}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}}-{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq 3d,

substituting which into (
C.9
) then gives us that

\sup_{\tau\in[0,h_{n}]}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(K)}-{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq 3d\left(\dfrac{h_{n}^{2}e^{h_{n}+\frac{3}{2}}L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}}{2}\right)^{K}+\frac{e^{h_{n}+\frac{3}{2}}h_{n}/2}{1-h_{n}^{2}e^{h_{n}+\frac{3}{2}}L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}/2}\Big(h_{n}\delta_{\infty}^{2}+\mathbb{E}[D_{t_{n}}]\Big),

as desired.
∎

Now it remains to bound
C_{t_{n}}
and
D_{t_{n}}
in Lemma
C.4
. We first bound
D_{t_{n}}
using the following lemma:

Lemma C.5
.

For any
n\in[0:N-1]
, we have that

\mathbb{E}\left[D_{t_{n}}\right]\lesssim d\epsilon^{2}h_{n}.

Proof.

For any
n\in[0:N-2]
, we have
T-t_{n+1}\gtrsim{\mathcal{O}}(1)
and thus by
[
111
, Corollary 1]
that

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{N-1}+\tau_{n,m}}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{N-1}+\tau_{n,m}})-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{N-1}+\tau^{\prime}}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{N-1}+\tau^{\prime}})\right\|^{2}\right]\lesssim d\epsilon_{n,m}^{2},

for any
\tau^{\prime}\in[\tau_{n,m},\tau_{n,m+1}]
,
and thus

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[D_{t_{n}}\right]=\int_{0}^{h_{n}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})})-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau^{\prime}}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau^{\prime}})\right\|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{m=0}^{M_{n}}\int_{\tau_{n,m}}^{\tau_{n,m+1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau_{n,m}}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau_{n,m}})-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern
