4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau^{\prime}}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau^{\prime}})\right\|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{m=0}^{M_{n}}d\epsilon_{n,m}^{2}\epsilon_{n,m}\leq d\epsilon^{2}h_{n}.

For
n=N-1
, notice that by the step size schedule (
cf.
Section
3.1.1
) and suppose
\epsilon\leq 1/2
, we have

\dfrac{T-\tau}{2}\leq T-g_{n}(\tau)\leq T-\tau,

and then again
[
111
, Corollary 1]
states

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\epsilon_{n,m}}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\epsilon_{n,m}})-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau^{\prime}}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau^{\prime}})\right\|^{2}\right]\lesssim\dfrac{d\epsilon_{n,m}^{2}}{T-\tau_{n,m}},

and thus

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[D_{t_{N-1}}\right]=\int_{0}^{h_{N-1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{N-1}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{N-1}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})})-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{N-1}+\tau^{\prime}}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{N-1}+\tau^{\prime}})\right\|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{m=0}^{M_{N-1}}\int_{\tau_{n,m}}^{\tau_{n,m+1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{N-1}+\tau_{n,m}}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{N-1}+g_{n}(\tau^{\prime})})-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{N-1}+\tau^{\prime}}({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{N-1}+\tau^{\prime}})\right\|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{m=0}^{M_{N-1}}\dfrac{d\epsilon_{n,m}^{2}}{T-\tau_{n,m}}\epsilon_{n,m}\leq\sum_{m=0}^{M_{N-1}}d\epsilon_{n,m}^{2}\epsilon\lesssim\int_{\delta_{\infty}}^{T-t_{N-1}}d\tau{\mathrm{d}}\tau\lesssim d\epsilon^{2}h_{N-1}.

∎

Remark C.6
.

The above lemma is able to achieve a better dependency on
\epsilon
compared to Lemma
B.7
, because the backward process
({\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t})_{t\in[0,T]}
is now a deterministic process in the probability flow ODE formulation, instead of a stochastic process as in the SDE formulation as in Lemma
B.7
. Thus, intuitively applying Cauchy-Schwarz rather than Itô symmetry gives us a
{\mathcal{O}}(\epsilon^{2})
-dependency rather than
{\mathcal{O}}(\epsilon)
-dependency.

Theorem C.7
.

Under Assumptions
3.1’
,
3.2
,
3.3
, and
3.4
, then the distribution
\widetilde{q}_{t_{n},h_{n}}
that the parallelized predictor step generates samples from satisfies the following error bound:

W_{2}(\widetilde{q}_{t_{n},h_{n}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}})^{2}\lesssim de^{-K}+h_{n}^{2}\delta_{\infty}^{2}+d\epsilon^{2}h_{n}^{2},

for
n\in[0:N-1]
.

Proof.

By the definition of 2-Wasserstein distance, we have for any coupling of
\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},h_{n}}^{(K)}
and
{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+h_{n}}
,

W_{2}(\widetilde{q}_{t_{n},h_{n}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}})^{2}\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},h_{n}}^{(K)}-{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+h_{n}}\right\|^{2}\right],

and therefore

\displaystyle W_{2}(\widetilde{q}_{t_{n},h_{n}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}})^{2}\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},h_{n}}^{(K)}-{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+h_{n}}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq\sup_{\tau\in[0,h_{n}]}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},\tau}^{(K)}-{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle{\bm{x}}$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}+\tau}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3d\left(\dfrac{h_{n}^{2}e^{h_{n}+\frac{3}{2}}L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}}{2}\right)^{K}+\frac{e^{h_{n}+\frac{3}{2}}h_{n}/2}{1-h_{n}^{2}e^{h_{n}+\frac{3}{2}}L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}/2}\Big(h_{n}\delta_{\infty}^{2}+\mathbb{E}[D_{t_{n}}]\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle de^{-K}+h_{n}^{2}\delta_{\infty}^{2}+d\epsilon^{2}h_{n}^{2},

where for the second to last inequality we used Lemma
C.4
, the last inequality is due to Lemma
C.5
and the assumption
h_{n}^{2}e^{h_{n}}L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}\ll 1
.
∎

C.3
Parallelized Corrector Step

After each predictor step, we run the corrector step for
{\mathcal{O}}(1)
time to reduce the error. Particularly, we apply the Parallelized underdamped Langevin dynamics algorithm
[
130
]
to the corrector step, which yields
{\mathcal{O}}(1)
approximate time complexity compared to the ordinary implementation of the ULMC dynamics as in
[
111
]
. In the following, we will drop the dependency on
\omega
for notational simplicity, and we refer readers to Appendix
A.2
and
B.2
to review the change of measure arguments and the application of Girsanov’s theorem
A.4
.
We will also use a general notation
\ast^{\dagger}
to distinguish the time in the backward process and the inner time in the corrector step of the
n
-th block.

We first define the true underdamped Langevin dynamics
({\bm{u}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}},{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}})_{t\geq 0}
:

\begin{cases}{\mathrm{d}}{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}}={\bm{v}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}}{\mathrm{d}}t^{\dagger}\\
{\mathrm{d}}{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}}=-\gamma{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}}{\mathrm{d}}t^{\dagger}-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}}({\bm{u}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}}){\mathrm{d}}t^{\dagger}+\sqrt{2\gamma}{\mathrm{d}}{\bm{b}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}},\end{cases}

(C.10)

with initial condition
{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},0}\equiv\widetilde{{\bm{y}}}_{t_{n},h_{n}}^{(K^{\dagger})}
from the predictor step and
{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},0}\sim{\mathcal{N}}(0,{\bm{I}}_{d})
, where
({\bm{b}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}})_{t\geq 0}
is a Wiener process.
We may also write the system of SDEs above in the following matrix form:

{\mathrm{d}}\left[\begin{matrix}{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}}\\
{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}}\end{matrix}\right]=\left[\left[\begin{matrix}{\bm{0}}&{\bm{I}}_{d}\\
{\bm{0}}&-\gamma{\bm{I}}_{d}\end{matrix}\right]\left[\begin{matrix}{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}}\\
{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}}\end{matrix}\right]-\left[\begin{matrix}{\bm{0}}\\
\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}}({\bm{u}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}})\end{matrix}\right]\right]{\mathrm{d}}t^{\dagger}+\left[\begin{matrix}{\bm{0}}&{\bm{0}}\\
{\bm{0}}&\sqrt{2\gamma}{\bm{I}}_{d}\end{matrix}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\left[\begin{matrix}{\bm{b}}^{\prime}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}}\\
{\bm{b}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}}\end{matrix}\right].

We run this underdamped Langevin dynamics until the pre-determined time horizon
T^{\dagger}
. We also define the joint probability distribution of
({\bm{u}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}},{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}})
at time
t
as
\pi_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}}({\bm{u}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}},{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}})
and its marginal on
{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}}
as
\pi_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}}^{{\bm{u}}}({\bm{u}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}})
.

Similar to the parallelizing strategy in Section
3.1.1
, we discretize the time interval
[0,T^{\dagger}]
into
N^{\dagger}
blocks with length
h^{\dagger}=T^{\dagger}/N^{\dagger}
. Within the
n
-th block, we further divide the block
[n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},(n+1)h^{\dagger}]
into
M^{\dagger}
steps, each with step size
\epsilon^{\dagger}=h^{\dagger}/M^{\dagger}
.

Definition C.8

(Auxiliary corrector process)
.

For any
n^{\dagger}\in[0:N^{\dagger}-1]
, we define the auxiliary corrector process
(\widehat{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k^{\dagger})})_{\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}]}
as the solution to the following SDE recursively for
k^{\dagger}\in[0:K^{\dagger}-1]
:

\begin{cases}{\mathrm{d}}\widehat{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k+1)}=\widehat{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k+1)}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger},\\
{\mathrm{d}}\widehat{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k+1)}=-\gamma\widehat{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k+1)}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}-{\bm{s}}_{t_{n+1}}\big(\widehat{\bm{u}}^{(k^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},g_{n}(\tau^{\dagger})}\big){\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}+\sqrt{2\gamma}{\mathrm{d}}{\bm{b}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}\end{cases}

(C.11)

with the initial condition

\begin{cases}\widehat{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(0)}\equiv\widehat{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}\\
\widehat{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(0)}=\widehat{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}\end{cases}\text{for}\ \tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}],\ \text{and}\ \begin{cases}\widehat{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k^{\dagger})}\equiv\widehat{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}\\
\widehat{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},0}^{(k^{\dagger})}\equiv\widehat{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}\end{cases}\text{for}\ k\in[1:K^{\dagger}],

(C.12)

where

\widehat{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}:=\widehat{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},(n^{\dagger}-1)h^{\dagger},h^{\dagger}}^{(K^{\dagger})},\quad\widehat{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}:=\widehat{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},(n^{\dagger}-1)h^{\dagger},h^{\dagger}}^{(K^{\dagger})}

for
n^{\dagger}\in[1:N^{\dagger}-1]
, and

\widehat{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},0}={\bm{y}}_{t_{n},h_{n}}^{(K)},\quad\widehat{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},0}\sim{\mathcal{N}}(0,{\bm{I}}_{d}).

We define the joint probability distribution of
(\widehat{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}},\widehat{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}})
at time
t
as
\widehat{\pi}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}}(\widehat{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}},\widehat{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}})
and its marginal on
\widehat{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}}
as
\widehat{\pi}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}}^{\widehat{\bm{u}}}(\widehat{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}})
. We will also denote the resulting probability distribution of
\widehat{\pi}_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}}^{\widehat{\bm{u}}}
as
\widehat{q}_{t_{n+1}}
.

Lemma C.9

(Equivalence between (
C.2
) and (
C.11
))
.

For any
n^{\dagger}\in[0:N^{\dagger}-1]
, the update rule in Algorithm
2
is equivalent to the exact solution of the auxiliary process (
C.11
) for any
k^{\dagger}\in[0:K^{\dagger}-1]
and
\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}]
.

Proof.

Without loss of generality, we will prove the lemma for
m^{\dagger}=M^{\dagger}
. The proof for
m^{\dagger}\in[0:M^{\dagger}-1]
can be done similarly.

We first rewrite (
C.2
) into the matrix form:

\displaystyle{\mathrm{d}}\left[\begin{matrix}\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k^{\dagger})}\\
\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k^{\dagger})}\end{matrix}\right]=

\displaystyle\left[\left[\begin{matrix}{\bm{0}}&{\bm{I}}_{d}\\
{\bm{0}}&-\gamma{\bm{I}}_{d}\end{matrix}\right]\left[\begin{matrix}\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k^{\dagger})}\\
\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k^{\dagger})}\end{matrix}\right]-\left[\begin{matrix}{\bm{0}}\\
{\bm{s}}_{t_{n+1}}\big(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(k^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},g_{n}(\tau^{\dagger})}\big)\end{matrix}\right]\right]{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}

(C.13)

\displaystyle+\left[\begin{matrix}{\bm{0}}&{\bm{0}}\\
{\bm{0}}&\sqrt{2\gamma}{\bm{I}}_{d}\end{matrix}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\left[\begin{matrix}{\bm{b}}^{\prime}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}\\
{\bm{b}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}\end{matrix}\right].

Define the time-dependent matrix
{\bm{G}}(\cdot)
as

{\bm{G}}(t^{\dagger}):=\left[\begin{matrix}{\bm{I}}_{d}&\frac{1-e^{-\gamma t^{\dagger}}}{\gamma}{\bm{I}}_{d}\\
{\bm{0}}&e^{-\gamma t^{\dagger}}{\bm{I}}_{d}\end{matrix}\right]=\exp\left(\left(\begin{matrix}{\bm{0}}&{\bm{I}}_{d}\\
{\bm{0}}&-\gamma{\bm{I}}_{d}\end{matrix}\right)t^{\dagger}\right),

(C.14)

satisfying that

\dfrac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t^{\dagger}}{\bm{G}}(t^{\dagger})=\left[\begin{matrix}{\bm{0}}&{\bm{I}}_{d}\\
{\bm{0}}&-\gamma{\bm{I}}_{d}\end{matrix}\right]{\bm{G}}(t^{\dagger})={\bm{G}}(t^{\dagger})\left[\begin{matrix}{\bm{0}}&{\bm{I}}_{d}\\
{\bm{0}}&-\gamma{\bm{I}}_{d}\end{matrix}\right].

Now we multiply
{\bm{G}}(-\tau^{\dagger})
on both sides of (
C.13
) to obtain:

\displaystyle{\mathrm{d}}\left({\bm{G}}(-\tau^{\dagger})\left[\begin{matrix}\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k^{\dagger})}\\
\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k^{\dagger})}\end{matrix}\right]\right)=

\displaystyle-{\bm{G}}(-\tau^{\dagger})\left[\begin{matrix}{\bm{0}}\\
{\bm{s}}_{t_{n+1}}\big(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(k^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},g_{n}(\tau^{\dagger})}\big)\end{matrix}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}

\displaystyle+{\bm{G}}(-\tau^{\dagger})\left[\begin{matrix}{\bm{0}}&{\bm{0}}\\
{\bm{0}}&\sqrt{2\gamma}{\bm{I}}_{d}\end{matrix}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\left[\begin{matrix}{\bm{b}}^{\prime}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}\\
{\bm{b}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}\end{matrix}\right].

Integrating on both sides from
0
to
h^{\dagger}
and multiplying
{\bm{G}}(h^{\dagger})
on both sides, we have

\displaystyle\left[\begin{matrix}\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau}^{(k^{\dagger})}\\
\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau}^{(k^{\dagger})}\end{matrix}\right]-{\bm{G}}(h^{\dagger})\left[\begin{matrix}\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},0}^{(k^{\dagger})}\\
\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},0}^{(k^{\dagger})}\end{matrix}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{h^{\dagger}}{\bm{G}}(h^{\dagger}-{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime})\left[\begin{matrix}{\bm{0}}\\
{\bm{s}}_{t_{n+1}}\big(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(k^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},g({\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime})}\big)\end{matrix}\right]{\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{h^{\dagger}}{\bm{G}}(h^{\dagger}-{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime})\left[\begin{matrix}{\bm{0}}&{\bm{0}}\\
{\bm{0}}&\sqrt{2\gamma}{\bm{I}}_{d}\end{matrix}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\left[\begin{matrix}{\bm{b}}^{\prime}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\\
{\bm{b}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\end{matrix}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{m^{\dagger}=0}^{M^{\dagger}-1}\int_{m^{\dagger}\epsilon^{\dagger}}^{(m^{\dagger}+1)\epsilon^{\dagger}}{\bm{G}}(h^{\dagger}-{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}){\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}\left[\begin{matrix}{\bm{0}}\\
{\bm{s}}_{t_{n+1}}\big(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(k^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},m^{\dagger}\epsilon^{\dagger}}\big)\end{matrix}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{m^{\dagger}=0}^{M^{\dagger}-1}\int_{m^{\dagger}\epsilon^{\dagger}}^{(m^{\dagger}+1)\epsilon^{\dagger}}{\bm{G}}(h^{\dagger}-{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime})\left[\begin{matrix}{\bm{0}}&{\bm{0}}\\
{\bm{0}}&\sqrt{2\gamma}{\bm{I}}_{d}\end{matrix}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\left[\begin{matrix}{\bm{b}}^{\prime}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\\
{\bm{b}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\end{matrix}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{m^{\dagger}=0}^{M^{\dagger}-1}\left({\bm{G}}(\epsilon^{\dagger})-{\bm{I}}_{d}\right){\bm{G}}((M^{\dagger}-m^{\dagger}-1)\epsilon^{\dagger})\left[\begin{matrix}{\bm{0}}\\
{\bm{s}}_{t_{n+1}}\big(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(k^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},m^{\dagger}\epsilon^{\dagger}}\big)\end{matrix}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{m^{\dagger}=0}^{M^{\dagger}-1}\int_{m^{\dagger}\epsilon^{\dagger}}^{(m^{\dagger}+1)\epsilon^{\dagger}}{\bm{G}}(h^{\dagger}-{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime})\left[\begin{matrix}{\bm{0}}&{\bm{0}}\\
{\bm{0}}&\sqrt{2\gamma}{\bm{I}}_{d}\end{matrix}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\left[\begin{matrix}{\bm{b}}^{\prime}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\\
{\bm{b}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\end{matrix}\right].

By Itô isometry, we have

\displaystyle\int_{m^{\dagger}\epsilon^{\dagger}}^{(m^{\dagger}+1)\epsilon^{\dagger}}{\bm{G}}(h^{\dagger}-{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime})\left[\begin{matrix}{\bm{0}}&{\bm{0}}\\
{\bm{0}}&\sqrt{2\gamma}{\bm{I}}_{d}\end{matrix}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\left[\begin{matrix}{\bm{b}}^{\prime}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\\
{\bm{b}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\end{matrix}\right]

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\mathcal{N}}\Bigg({\bm{0}},\left[\begin{matrix}{\bm{0}}&{\bm{0}}\\
{\bm{0}}&\sqrt{2\gamma{\bm{I}}_{d}}\end{matrix}\right]{\bm{G}}((M^{\dagger}-m^{\dagger}-1)\epsilon^{\dagger})^{\top}\left({\bm{G}}(\epsilon^{\dagger})-{\bm{I}}_{d}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle\left({\bm{G}}(\epsilon^{\dagger})-{\bm{I}}_{d}\right){\bm{G}}((M^{\dagger}-m^{\dagger}-1)\epsilon^{\dagger})\left[\begin{matrix}{\bm{0}}&{\bm{0}}\\
{\bm{0}}&\sqrt{2\gamma{\bm{I}}_{d}}\end{matrix}\right]\Bigg)

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle\left[\begin{matrix}{\bm{0}}\\
{\mathcal{N}}\left({\bm{0}},2\gamma(1+\gamma^{-2})(1-e^{-\gamma\epsilon^{\dagger}})^{2}e^{-2\gamma(M^{\dagger}-m^{\dagger}+1)\epsilon^{\dagger})}{\bm{I}}_{d}\right)\end{matrix}\right],

as desired
∎

Definition C.10

(Interpolating corrector process)
.

For any
n^{\dagger}\in[0:N^{\dagger}-1]
, we define the interpolating corrector process
(\widehat{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k^{\dagger})})_{\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}]}
as the solution to the following SDE recursively for
k^{\dagger}\in[0:K^{\dagger}-1]
:

\begin{cases}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k+1)}=\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k+1)}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger},\\
{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k+1)}=-\gamma\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k+1)}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}-{\bm{s}}_{t_{n+1}}\big(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(k^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},g_{n}(\tau^{\dagger})}\big){\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}+\sqrt{2\gamma}{\mathrm{d}}{\bm{b}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}\end{cases}

(C.15)

with the initial condition

\begin{cases}\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(0)}\equiv\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}\\
\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(0)}=\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}\end{cases}\text{for}\ \tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}],\ \text{and}\ \begin{cases}\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k^{\dagger})}\equiv\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}\\
\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},0}^{(k^{\dagger})}\equiv\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}\end{cases}\text{for}\ k\in[1:K^{\dagger}],

(C.16)

where

\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}:=\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{(n^{\dagger}-1)h^{\dagger},h^{\dagger}}^{(K^{\dagger})},\quad\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}:=\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{(n^{\dagger}-1)h^{\dagger},h^{\dagger}}^{(K^{\dagger})}

for
n^{\dagger}\in[1:N^{\dagger}-1]
, and

\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},0}=\widetilde{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},h_{n}}^{(K)},\quad\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},0}\sim{\mathcal{N}}(0,{\bm{I}}_{d}).

We define the joint probability distribution of
(\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}},\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}})
at time
t
as
\widetilde{\pi}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}}(\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}},\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}})
and its marginal on
\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}}
as
\widetilde{\pi}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}}^{\widetilde{\bm{u}}}(\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}})
.

We invoke Girsanov’s theorem (Theorem
A.4
) again by the following procedure

1.

Setting (
A.2
) as the auxiliary process (
C.15
) at iteration
K^{\dagger}
, where
{\bm{b}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}}(\omega)
is a Wiener process under the measure
Q
;

2.

Defining another process
\widetilde{{\bm{b}}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}
governed by the following SDE:

{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{{\bm{b}}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}={\mathrm{d}}{\bm{b}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}-{\bm{\phi}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}(\tau^{\dagger}){\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger},

(C.17)

where

{\bm{\phi}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}(\tau^{\dagger})=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\gamma}}\left({\bm{s}}_{t_{n+1}}(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger}-1)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}})-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}}(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}})\right)

(C.18)

and computing the Radon-Nikodym derivative of the measure
P
with respect to
Q
as

\frac{{\mathrm{d}}P}{{\mathrm{d}}Q}=\exp\left(\int_{0}^{h^{\dagger}}{\bm{\phi}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}(\tau^{\dagger})^{\top}{\mathrm{d}}{\bm{b}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}-\dfrac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{h^{\dagger}}\|{\bm{\phi}}_{nh}(\tau^{\dagger})\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}\right);

(C.19)

3.

Concluding that (
C.15
) at iteration
K^{\dagger}
under the measure
Q

satisfies the following SDE:

\begin{cases}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(K^{\dagger})}=\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(K^{\dagger})}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}\\
{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(K^{\dagger})}=-\gamma\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(K^{\dagger})}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}}(\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(K^{\dagger})}){\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}+\sqrt{2\gamma}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\bm{b}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}},\end{cases}

(C.20)

with
(\widetilde{\bm{b}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}})_{\tau^{\dagger}\geq 0}
being a Wiener process under the measure
P
. If we replace
(\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(K^{\dagger})},\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(K^{\dagger})})
by
({\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}},{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}})
, one should notice (
C.20
) is immediately the original backward SDE (
C.10
) with the true score function on
t\in[n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},(n+1)h^{\dagger}]
:

\begin{cases}{\mathrm{d}}{{\bm{u}}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}={{\bm{v}}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}\\
{\mathrm{d}}{{\bm{v}}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}=-\gamma{{\bm{v}}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}}({{\bm{u}}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}){\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}+\sqrt{2\gamma}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\bm{b}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}.\end{cases}

(C.21)

We further define the joint probability distribution of
({\bm{u}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}},{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}})
at time
t
as
\pi_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}}({\bm{u}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}},{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}})
and its marginal on
{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}}
as
\pi_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}}^{{\bm{u}}}({\bm{u}}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}})
.

Remark C.11
.

The application of Girsanov’s theorem
A.4
is by writing the system of SDEs in the matrix form.

Definition C.12

(Stationary process)
.

Under the
P
-measure that is defined by the Radon-Nikodym derivative (
C.19
),
we may define a stationary underdamped Langevin process for
n^{\dagger}\in[0:N^{\dagger}-1]
and
\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}]
as

\begin{cases}{\mathrm{d}}{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}^{\ast}={\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}^{\ast}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger},\\
{\mathrm{d}}{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}^{\ast}=-\gamma{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}^{\ast}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}}({\bm{u}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}^{\ast}){\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}+\sqrt{2\gamma}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\bm{b}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}},\end{cases}

(C.22)

with the initial condition
{\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}\sim{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}}
and
{\bm{v}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}\sim{\mathcal{N}}(0,{\bm{I}}_{d})
. We define the joint probability distribution of
({\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}},{\bm{v}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}})
at time
t
as
\pi^{\ast}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}}({\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}},{\bm{v}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}})
and its marginal on
{\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}}
as
\pi_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}}^{\ast,{\bm{u}}^{\ast}}({\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},t^{\dagger}})
.

Thus, from Corollary
A.5
, we have
that

\displaystyle D_{\mathrm{KL}}(\pi_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}\|\widetilde{\pi}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}})

(C.23)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle D_{\mathrm{KL}}(\pi_{t_{n},(n-1)h^{\dagger}}\|\widetilde{\pi}_{t_{n},(n-1)h^{\dagger}})+\sum_{n=0}^{N^{\dagger}-1}D_{\mathrm{KL}}(\pi_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}:(n+1)h^{\dagger}}\|\widetilde{\pi}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}:(n+1)h^{\dagger}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle D_{\mathrm{KL}}(\pi_{t_{n},(n-1)h^{\dagger}}\|\widetilde{\pi}_{t_{n},(n-1)h^{\dagger}})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4\gamma}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\int_{0}^{h^{\dagger}}\left\|{\bm{s}}_{t_{n+1}}(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger}-1)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}})-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}}(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}})\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}\right].

By triangle inequality, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{h^{\dagger}}\left\|{\bm{s}}_{t_{n+1}}(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger}-1)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}})-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}}(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}})\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}

(C.24)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 5\int_{0}^{h^{\dagger}}\left\|{\bm{s}}_{t_{n+1}}(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger}-1)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}})-{\bm{s}}_{t_{n+1}}(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}})\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 5\int_{0}^{h^{\dagger}}\left\|{\bm{s}}_{t_{n+1}}(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}})-{\bm{s}}_{t_{n+1}}({\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}})\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 5\int_{0}^{h^{\dagger}}\left\|{\bm{s}}_{t_{n+1}}({\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}})-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}}({\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}})\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 5\int_{0}^{h^{\dagger}}\left\|\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}}({\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}})-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}}({\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}})\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 5\int_{0}^{h^{\dagger}}\left\|\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}}({\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}})-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}}(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}})\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 5L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}\int_{0}^{h^{\dagger}}\left\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger}-1)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}}-\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}}\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 5\underbrace{\left(L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}\int_{0}^{h^{\dagger}}\left\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}}-{\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}}\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}+L_{p}^{2}\int_{0}^{h^{\dagger}}\left\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}-{\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}\right)}_{:=E_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 5h^{\dagger}\delta_{\infty}^{2}+5L_{p}^{2}\underbrace{\int_{0}^{h^{\dagger}}\left\|{\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}}-{\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}}_{:=F_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}},

where we used the Lipschitz continuity of the learned score function (Assumption
3.3
) and the true score function (Assumption
3.4
), and the
\delta_{\infty}
-accuracy of the learned score function at each time step (Assumption
3.1’
).

Now we proceed to bound the terms in the error decomposition (
C.24
). We first bound the
F_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}
term by the following lemma:

Lemma C.13
.

For any
n\in[0:N-1]
and
\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}]
, we have

\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\left\|{\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}}-{\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq d{\epsilon^{\dagger}}^{2},

and therefore

\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[F_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}\right]\leq dh^{\dagger}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}^{2}.

Proof.

By the definition of
({\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n}.n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau},{\bm{v}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau})
as the stationary underdamped Langevin dynamics (
C.22
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\left\|{\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}}-{\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\left\|\int_{{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}}^{\tau^{\dagger}}{\bm{v}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}{\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon^{\dagger}\int_{{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}}^{\tau^{\dagger}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\left\|{\bm{v}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\right\|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}\leq d{\epsilon^{\dagger}}^{2},

where the first inequality follows from Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and the last inequality is by the fact that

{\bm{v}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\sim{\mathcal{N}}(0,{\bm{I}}_{d}),\quad\text{for any}\ {\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}\in[0,h^{\dagger}].

Consequently, we have

\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[F_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}\right]=\int_{0}^{h^{\dagger}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\left\|{\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}}-{\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}\right\|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}\leq dh^{\dagger}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}^{2}.

∎

The term
E_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}
can be bounded with the following lemma:

Lemma C.14
.

For any
n^{\dagger}\in[0:N^{\dagger}-1]
, suppose that
\gamma\lesssim L_{p}^{-1/2}
and
T^{\dagger}\lesssim L_{p}^{-1/2}
, then we have the following inequality for any
\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}]

\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\left\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}-{\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}\right\|^{2}\right]\lesssim W_{2}^{2}(\widetilde{q}_{t_{n},h_{n}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}}),

and therefore

\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[E_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}\right]\lesssim h^{\dagger}(L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}+L_{p}^{2})W_{2}^{2}(\widetilde{q}_{t_{n},h_{n}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}}).

Proof.

Recall that under the measure
P
,
\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}
follows the dynamics of
{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}
(
C.21
) for
\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}]
, which coincides with that of
{\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}
. As the only difference between the two processes
{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}
and
{\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}
is the initial condition, we can invoke Lemma 10 proved in
[
111
]
to deduce that

\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\left\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}-{\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}\right\|^{2}\right]\lesssim W_{2}^{2}(\pi_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}}),

where the assumption that
\gamma\lesssim L_{p}^{-1/2}
and
T^{\dagger}\lesssim L_{p}^{-1/2}
is required.

Now notice that
{\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}
and
{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}
also follow the same dynamics with the true score function for
\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}]
, for any coupling of
{\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}
and
{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}
, we have

\displaystyle W_{2}^{2}(\pi_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}})\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}-{\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle W_{2}^{2}(\pi_{t_{n},0},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}})=W_{2}^{2}(\widetilde{q}_{t_{n},h_{n}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}}),

where the last equality is again by
[
111
, Lemma 10]
.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[E_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{h^{\dagger}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}\left\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}}-{\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}}\right\|^{2}+L_{p}^{2}\left\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}-{\bm{u}}^{\ast}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}\right\|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle h^{\dagger}(L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}+L_{p}^{2})W_{2}^{2}(\widetilde{q}_{t_{n},h_{n}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}}).

∎

Now, we provide lemmas that are used to bound the first term in (
C.24
).

Lemma C.15
.

For any
n^{\dagger}\in[0:N^{\dagger}-1]
, we have the following estimate:

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}]}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\left\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(1)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}-\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(0)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{5L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}h^{\dagger}e^{(3+\gamma)h^{\dagger}}}{2\gamma}\sup_{\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}]}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\left\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger}-1)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}-\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\dfrac{5{h^{\dagger}}e^{(3+\gamma)h^{\dagger}}}{2\gamma}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[E_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}+h^{\dagger}\delta_{\infty}^{2}+L_{p}^{2}F_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}\right]+{h^{\dagger}}^{2}e^{(3+\gamma)h^{\dagger}}\left(3\gamma d+M_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}\right)+h^{\dagger}e^{2h^{\dagger}}d.

Proof.

Let
{\bm{\mu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}:=\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(1)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}-\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(0)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}
and
{\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}:=\widetilde{\bm{v}}^{(1)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}-\widetilde{\bm{v}}^{(0)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}
. Then for
k=0
, we may rewrite (
C.15
) as follows

\begin{cases}{\mathrm{d}}{\bm{\mu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}=\left({\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}+\widetilde{\bm{v}}^{(0)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}\right){\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}\\
{\mathrm{d}}{\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}=-\gamma({\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}+\widetilde{\bm{v}}^{(0)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}){\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}-{\bm{s}}_{t_{n+1}}(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(0)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}){\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}+\sqrt{2\gamma}{\mathrm{d}}{\bm{b}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}\end{cases}

(C.25)

On the one hand, by using the first equation in (
C.25
), we may compute the derivative

\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\right\|^{2}=2{\bm{\mu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}^{\top}\left({\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}+\widetilde{\bm{v}}^{(0)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\right)

and integrate it for
{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}\in[0,\tau^{\dagger}]
, which yields

\displaystyle\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}\right\|^{2}=2\int_{0}^{\tau^{\dagger}}{\bm{\mu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}^{\top}({\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}+\widetilde{\bm{v}}^{(0)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}){\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\int_{0}^{\tau^{\dagger}}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{\tau^{\dagger}}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{\tau^{\dagger}}\left\|\widetilde{\bm{v}}^{(0)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}.

Applying Gronwall’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}\right\|^{2}\leq e^{2\tau^{\dagger}}\left(\int_{0}^{\tau^{\dagger}}\left\|{\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{\tau^{\dagger}}\left\|\widetilde{\bm{v}}^{(0)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}\right).

We then take expectation with respect to the path measure
P
and then the supremum with respect to
\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}]
, implying that

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}]}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}\right\|^{2}\right]

(C.26)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}]}\left(e^{2\tau^{\dagger}}\int_{0}^{\tau^{\dagger}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\left\|{\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\right\|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}+e^{2\tau^{\dagger}}\int_{0}^{\tau^{\dagger}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\left\|\widetilde{\bm{v}}^{(0)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\right\|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle h^{\dagger}e^{2h^{\dagger}}\sup_{\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}]}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\left\|{\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\right\|^{2}\right]+h^{\dagger}e^{2h^{\dagger}}d.

On the other hand, by applying Itô’s lemma and plugging in the expression of
{\bm{b}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}
given by (
C.17
), we have

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\mathrm{d}}\|{\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}\|^{2}

(C.27)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\Bigg[2\gamma\|{\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}\|^{2}+2\gamma{\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{v}}^{(0)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}+2{\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{\top}{\bm{s}}_{t_{n+1}}\Big(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(0)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}\Big)-2\gamma d\Bigg]{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 2{\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{\top}\sqrt{2\gamma}\big({\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\bm{b}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+\tau^{\dagger}}+{\bm{\phi}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}(\tau^{\dagger}){\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}\big),

Then similarly, we may compute the derivative of
\|{\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}\|^{2}
, integrate it for
\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}]
, and take the supremum with respect to
\tau^{\dagger}
to obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\|{\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[-\int_{0}^{\tau^{\dagger}}\Bigg(2\gamma\|{\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\|^{2}+2\gamma{\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{v}}^{(0)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}-2\gamma d\Bigg){\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[-\int_{0}^{\tau^{\dagger}}2{\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}^{\top}{\bm{s}}_{t_{n+1}}\Big(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(0)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\Big){\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 2\sqrt{2\gamma}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\int_{0}^{\tau^{\dagger}}{\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}^{\top}\Big({\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\bm{b}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}+{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}+{\bm{\phi}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}({\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}){\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}\Big)\right].

By Itô’s lemma, this equals to

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\|{\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[-\int_{0}^{\tau^{\dagger}}\Bigg(2\gamma\|{\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\|^{2}+2\gamma{\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{v}}^{(0)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}-2\gamma d\Bigg){\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[-\int_{0}^{\tau^{\dagger}}2{\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}^{\top}{\bm{s}}_{t_{n+1}}\Big(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(0)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\Big)+2\sqrt{2\gamma}{\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}^{\top}{\bm{\phi}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}({\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}){\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}\right].

Applying AM-GM gives

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\|{\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\tau^{\dagger}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[(1+\gamma)\|{\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\|^{2}+\|{\bm{\phi}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}({\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime})\|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\tau^{\dagger}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\gamma\left\|\widetilde{\bm{v}}^{(0)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\right\|^{2}+\left\|{\bm{s}}_{t_{n+1}}\Big(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(0)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\Big)\right\|^{2}+2\gamma d\right]{\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\tau^{\dagger}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[(1+\gamma)\|{\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\|^{2}+\|{\bm{\phi}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}({\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime})\|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}+\left(\gamma\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},0}^{(0)}\right\|^{2}\right]+M_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}+2\gamma d\right)\tau^{\dagger}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1+\gamma)\int_{0}^{\tau^{\dagger}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\|{\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{\tau^{\dagger}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\|{\bm{\phi}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}({\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime})\|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}+\tau^{\dagger}\left(3\gamma d+M_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}\right),

where in the last equality, we used the initialization of the auxiliary corrector process
\widetilde{\bm{v}}^{(0)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},0}\sim{\mathcal{N}}(0,{\bm{I}}_{d})
.

Again, we apply Gronwall’s inequality to the above inequality and take the supremum with respect to
\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}]
to obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}]}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\|{\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}\|^{2}\right]

(C.28)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle e^{(1+\gamma)h^{\dagger}}\int_{0}^{h^{\dagger}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\|{\bm{\phi}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}({\tau^{\dagger}})\|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}+h^{\dagger}e^{(1+\gamma)h^{\dagger}}\left(3\gamma d+M_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{e^{(1+\gamma)h^{\dagger}}}{2\gamma}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\int_{0}^{h^{\dagger}}\left\|{\bm{s}}_{t_{n+1}}(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger}-1)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\lfloor\frac{{\tau^{\dagger}}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}})-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}}(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}})\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}\right]

\displaystyle+h^{\dagger}e^{(1+\gamma)h^{\dagger}}\left(3\gamma d+M_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}\right),

and for the difference term within the expectation, we decompose it again by the triangle inequality in (
C.24
),
i.e.

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{h^{\dagger}}\left\|{\bm{s}}_{t_{n+1}}(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger}-1)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}})-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}}(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}})\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 5L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}\int_{0}^{h^{\dagger}}\left\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger}-1)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}}-\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}}\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}+5E_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}+5h^{\dagger}\delta_{\infty}^{2}+5L_{p}^{2}F_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}},

to obtain that

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}]}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\|{\bm{\nu}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{5L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}e^{(1+\gamma)h^{\dagger}}}{2\gamma}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\int_{0}^{h^{\dagger}}\left\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger}-1)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}}-\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}}\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\dfrac{5e^{(1+\gamma)h^{\dagger}}}{2\gamma}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[E_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}+h^{\dagger}\delta_{\infty}^{2}+L_{p}^{2}F_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}\right]+h^{\dagger}e^{(1+\gamma)h^{\dagger}}\left(3\gamma d+M_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{5L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}e^{(1+\gamma)h^{\dagger}}}{2\gamma}h^{\dagger}\sup_{\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}]}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\left\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger}-1)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}}-\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\dfrac{5e^{(1+\gamma)h^{\dagger}}}{2\gamma}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[E_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}+h^{\dagger}\delta_{\infty}^{2}+L_{p}^{2}F_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}\right]+h^{\dagger}e^{(1+\gamma)h^{\dagger}}\left(3\gamma d+M_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}\right),

substituting which into (
C.26
) completes our proof of this Lemma.
∎

Lemma C.16

(Exponential convergence of Picard iteration in the corrector step of PIADM-ODE)
.

For any
n^{\dagger}\in[0,N^{\dagger}-1]
, then the two ending terms
\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(K^{\dagger})}
and
\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(K^{\dagger})}
of the sequence
\{\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k^{\dagger})}\}_{k^{\dagger}\in[0:K^{\dagger}-1]}
satisfy the following exponential convergence rate

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}]}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\left\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(K^{\dagger})}-\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(K^{\dagger}-1)}\right\|^{2}\right]

(C.29)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{K^{\dagger}}\left(\dfrac{5{h^{\dagger}}e^{(3+\gamma)h^{\dagger}}}{2\gamma}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[E_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}+h^{\dagger}\delta_{\infty}^{2}+L_{p}^{2}F_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}\right]+{h^{\dagger}}^{2}e^{(3+\gamma)h^{\dagger}}\left(3\gamma d+M_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}\right)+h^{\dagger}e^{2h^{\dagger}}d\right),

where the coefficient

C_{K^{\dagger}}=\left(\dfrac{L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}{h^{\dagger}}^{2}e^{h^{\dagger}}}{2\gamma}\right)^{K^{\dagger}-1}\Bigg/\left(1-\dfrac{5L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}h^{\dagger}e^{(3+\gamma)h^{\dagger}}}{2\gamma}\left(\dfrac{L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}{h^{\dagger}}^{2}e^{h^{\dagger}}}{2\gamma}\right)^{K^{\dagger}-1}\right).

Proof.

We subtract the dynamics of
\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k+1)}
and
\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k)}
in (
C.15
) to obtain

{\mathrm{d}}\left(\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k+1)}-\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k^{\dagger})}\right)=\left(\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k+1)}-\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k^{\dagger})}\right){\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}.

Then, we use the formula above to compute the derivative

\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}\left\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}^{(k+1)}-\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}^{(k^{\dagger})}\right\|^{2}=2\left(\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}^{(k+1)}-\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}^{(k^{\dagger})}\right)^{\top}\left(\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}^{(k+1)}-\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}^{(k^{\dagger})}\right)

and integrate for
{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}\in[0,\tau^{\dagger}]
to obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k+1)}-\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k^{\dagger})}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\int_{0}^{\tau^{\dagger}}\left(\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}^{(k+1)}-\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}^{(k^{\dagger})}\right)^{\top}\left(\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}^{(k+1)}-\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}^{(k^{\dagger})}\right){\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\tau^{\dagger}}\left\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}^{(k+1)}-\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}^{(k^{\dagger})}\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{\tau^{\dagger}}\left\|\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}^{(k+1)}-\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}^{(k^{\dagger})}\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}

Applying Grönwall’s inequality gives us that

\displaystyle\left\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k+1)}-\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k^{\dagger})}\right\|^{2}\leq e^{\tau^{\dagger}}\int_{0}^{\tau^{\dagger}}\left\|\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}^{(k+1)}-\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}^{(k^{\dagger})}\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}

and taking the supremum with respect to
\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}]
on both sides above implies

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}]}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\left\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k+1)}-\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k^{\dagger})}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq h^{\dagger}e^{h^{\dagger}}\sup_{\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}]}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\left\|\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}^{(k+1)}-\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}^{(k^{\dagger})}\right\|^{2}\right].

(C.30)

We then apply a similar argument for
\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k+1)}-\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k^{\dagger})}
as well

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\mathrm{d}}\left(\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k+1)}-\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k^{\dagger})}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\gamma\left(\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k+1)}-\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k^{\dagger})}\right){\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}-\left({\bm{s}}_{t_{n+1}}(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(k^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}})-{\bm{s}}_{t_{n+1}}(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(k-1)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}})\right){\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger},

integrate which for
{\tau^{\dagger}}\in[0,\tau^{\dagger}]
to obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k+1)}-\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k^{\dagger})}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{\tau^{\dagger}}2\gamma\left\|\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}^{(k+1)}-\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}^{(k^{\dagger})}\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle 2\int_{0}^{\tau^{\dagger}}\left(\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}^{(k+1)}-\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}^{(k^{\dagger})}\right)^{\top}\left({\bm{s}}_{t_{n+1}}(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(k^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\lfloor\frac{{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}})-{\bm{s}}_{t_{n+1}}(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(k-1)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\lfloor\frac{{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}})\right){\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{2\gamma}\int_{0}^{\tau^{\dagger}}\left\|{\bm{s}}_{t_{n+1}}(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(k^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\lfloor\frac{{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}})-{\bm{s}}_{t_{n+1}}(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(k-1)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\lfloor\frac{{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}})\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}}{2\gamma}\int_{0}^{\tau^{\dagger}}\left\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(k^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\lfloor\frac{{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}}-\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(k-1)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\lfloor\frac{{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}}\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}{\tau^{\dagger}}^{\prime}.

And then taking the supremum with respect to
\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}]
on both sides above implies

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}]}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\left\|\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k+1)}-\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(k^{\dagger})}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq\dfrac{h^{\dagger}L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}}{2\gamma}\sup_{\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}]}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\left\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(k^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}-\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(k-1)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}\right\|^{2}\right]

(C.31)

Substituting (
C.31
) into (
C.30
) and iterating over
k\in[1:K^{\dagger}-1]
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}]}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\left\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(K^{\dagger})}-\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}^{(K^{\dagger}-1)}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq\dfrac{L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}{h^{\dagger}}^{2}e^{h^{\dagger}}}{2\gamma}\sup_{\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}]}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\left\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger}-1)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}-\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger}-2)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\dfrac{L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}{h^{\dagger}}^{2}e^{h^{\dagger}}}{2\gamma}\right)^{K^{\dagger}-1}\sup_{\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}]}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\left\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(1)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}-\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(0)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\dfrac{L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}{h^{\dagger}}^{2}e^{h^{\dagger}}}{2\gamma}\right)^{K^{\dagger}-1}\dfrac{5{h^{\dagger}}e^{(3+\gamma)h^{\dagger}}}{2\gamma}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[E_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}+h^{\dagger}\delta_{\infty}^{2}+L_{p}^{2}F_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\left(\dfrac{L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}{h^{\dagger}}^{2}e^{h^{\dagger}}}{2\gamma}\right)^{K^{\dagger}-1}\left({h^{\dagger}}^{2}e^{(3+\gamma)h^{\dagger}}\left(3\gamma d+M_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}\right)+h^{\dagger}e^{2h^{\dagger}}d\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\left(\dfrac{L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}{h^{\dagger}}^{2}e^{h^{\dagger}}}{2\gamma}\right)^{K^{\dagger}-1}\dfrac{5L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}h^{\dagger}e^{(3+\gamma)h^{\dagger}}}{2\gamma}\sup_{\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}]}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\left\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger}-1)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}-\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}}\right\|^{2}\right],

where we plug in the results from Lemma
C.15
in the last inequality. Rearranging the inequality above completes our proof.
∎

Theorem C.17
.

Under Assumptions
3.1’
,
3.2
,
3.3
, and
3.4
, given the following choices of the order of the parameters

\displaystyle T^{\dagger}={\mathcal{O}}(1),\quad N^{\dagger}={\mathcal{O}}(1),\quad h^{\dagger}=\Theta(1)

\displaystyle M^{\dagger}=\Theta(d^{1/2}\delta^{-1}),\quad\epsilon^{\dagger}=\Theta(d^{-1/2}\delta),\quad K^{\dagger}={\mathcal{O}}(\log(d\delta^{-2}))

and let
