\displaystyle\dfrac{L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}{h^{\dagger}}^{2}e^{h^{\dagger}}}{2\gamma}\ll 1,\quad\gamma\lesssim L_{p}^{-1/2},\quad T^{\dagger}\lesssim L_{p}^{-1/2}\wedge L_{{\bm{s}}}^{-1/2},\quad\delta_{\infty}\lesssim\delta

then the distribution
\widetilde{\pi}_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}}
satisfies the following error bound:

\displaystyle D_{\mathrm{KL}}(\pi_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}}\|\widetilde{\pi}_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}})\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{\dagger}W_{2}^{2}(\widetilde{q}_{t_{n},h_{n}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}})+T^{\dagger}\delta_{\infty}^{2}+dT^{\dagger}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}^{2}+e^{-K^{\dagger}}T^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}d

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle W_{2}^{2}(\widetilde{q}_{t_{n},h_{n}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}})+\delta^{2},

with a total of
K^{\dagger}N^{\dagger}={{\mathcal{O}}}\left(\log(d\delta^{-2})\right)
approximate time complexity and
M=\Theta\left(d^{1/2}\delta^{-2}\right)
space complexity for parallalizable
\delta
-accurate score function computations.

Proof.

Now, we continue the computation by plugging the decomposition in (
C.24
) and all the error bounds derived above into the equation. First for the last term in (
C.23
)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\int_{0}^{h^{\dagger}}\left\|{\bm{s}}_{t_{n+1}}(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger}-1)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}})-\nabla\log{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}}(\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}})\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\dagger}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 5L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}h^{\dagger}\sup_{\tau^{\dagger}\in[0,h^{\dagger}]}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\left\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger}-1)}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}}-\widetilde{\bm{u}}^{(K^{\dagger})}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},{\lfloor\frac{\tau^{\dagger}}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}\rfloor\epsilon^{\dagger}}}\right\|^{2}\right]+5\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[E_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}+h^{\dagger}\delta_{\infty}^{2}+L_{p}^{2}F_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 5\left(1+L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}h^{\dagger}C_{K^{\dagger}}\dfrac{5{h^{\dagger}}e^{(3+\gamma)h^{\dagger}}}{2\gamma}\right)\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[E_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}+h^{\dagger}\delta_{\infty}^{2}+L_{p}^{2}F_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 5L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}h^{\dagger}C_{K^{\dagger}}\left({h^{\dagger}}^{2}e^{(3+\gamma)h^{\dagger}}\left(3\gamma d+M_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}\right)+h^{\dagger}e^{2h^{\dagger}}d\right),

where the last inequality is by Lemma
C.16
. We further substitute Lemma
C.14
and
C.13
into (
C.23
) to obtain

\displaystyle D_{\mathrm{KL}}(\pi_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}\|\widetilde{\pi}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle D_{\mathrm{KL}}(\pi_{t_{n},(n-1)h^{\dagger}}\|\widetilde{\pi}_{t_{n},(n-1)h^{\dagger}})+5\dfrac{2\gamma+L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}C_{K^{\dagger}}5{h^{\dagger}}^{2}e^{(3+\gamma)h^{\dagger}}}{4\gamma^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[E_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}+h^{\dagger}\delta_{\infty}^{2}+L_{p}^{2}F_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\dfrac{5L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}h^{\dagger}C_{K^{\dagger}}}{2\gamma}\left({h^{\dagger}}^{2}e^{(3+\gamma)h^{\dagger}}\left(3\gamma d+M_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}\right)+h^{\dagger}e^{2h^{\dagger}}d\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle D_{\mathrm{KL}}(\pi_{t_{n},(n-1)h^{\dagger}}\|\widetilde{\pi}_{t_{n},(n-1)h^{\dagger}})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 5\dfrac{2\gamma+L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}C_{K^{\dagger}}5{h^{\dagger}}^{2}e^{(3+\gamma)h^{\dagger}}}{4\gamma^{2}}\left(h^{\dagger}(L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}+L_{p}^{2})W_{2}^{2}(\widetilde{q}_{t_{n},h_{n}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}})+h^{\dagger}\delta_{\infty}^{2}+dh^{\dagger}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\dfrac{5L_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}h^{\dagger}C_{K^{\dagger}}}{2\gamma}\left({h^{\dagger}}^{2}e^{(3+\gamma)h^{\dagger}}\left(3\gamma d+M_{{\bm{s}}}^{2}\right)+h^{\dagger}e^{2h^{\dagger}}d\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle D_{\mathrm{KL}}(\pi_{t_{n},(n-1)h^{\dagger}}\|\widetilde{\pi}_{t_{n},(n-1)h^{\dagger}})+h^{\dagger}W_{2}^{2}(\widetilde{q}_{t_{n},h_{n}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}})+h^{\dagger}\delta_{\infty}^{2}+dh^{\dagger}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}^{2}+e^{-K^{\dagger}}{h^{\dagger}}^{2}d,

and then sum over
n
to obtain

\displaystyle D_{\mathrm{KL}}(\pi_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}}\|\widetilde{\pi}_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}})=D_{\mathrm{KL}}(\pi_{t_{n},N^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}\|\widetilde{\pi}_{t_{n},N^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle D_{\mathrm{KL}}(\pi_{t_{n},0}\|\widetilde{\pi}_{t_{n},0})+N^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}W_{2}^{2}(\widetilde{q}_{t_{n},h_{n}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}})+N^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}\delta_{\infty}^{2}+dN^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}^{2}+e^{-K^{\dagger}}N^{\dagger}{h^{\dagger}}^{2}d

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{\dagger}W_{2}^{2}(\widetilde{q}_{t_{n},h_{n}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}})+T^{\dagger}\delta_{\infty}^{2}+dT^{\dagger}{\epsilon^{\dagger}}^{2}+e^{-K^{\dagger}}T^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}d.

Then, it is straightforward to see that when the following order of the parameters holds

\displaystyle T^{\dagger}={\mathcal{O}}(1),\quad h^{\dagger}=\Theta(1),\quad N^{\dagger}={\mathcal{O}}(1),

\displaystyle\epsilon^{\dagger}=\Theta(d^{-1/2}\delta),\quad M^{\dagger}={\mathcal{O}}(d^{1/2}\delta^{-1}),\quad K^{\dagger}={\mathcal{O}}(\log(d\delta^{-2}))

and
\delta_{\infty}\leq\delta
, we have

D_{\mathrm{KL}}(\pi_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}}\|\widetilde{\pi}_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}})\lesssim W_{2}^{2}(\widetilde{q}_{t_{n},h_{n}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}})+\delta^{2}.

∎

Lemma C.18
.

Suppose
T^{\dagger}\lesssim L_{p}^{-1/2}
, then we have

\mathrm{TV}(\pi_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}})\leq\sqrt{D_{\mathrm{KL}}(\pi_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}}\|{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{L_{p}^{\frac{1}{4}}(T^{\dagger})^{\frac{3}{2}}}W_{2}(\pi_{t_{n},0},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}})\lesssim W_{2}(\widetilde{q}_{t_{n},h_{n}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}}).

Proof.

A complete proof of the Lemma above is presented in
[
111
, Lemma 9]
, which is derived based on
[
145
, Corollary 4.7 (1) ]
.
∎

C.4
Overall Error Bound

We are now ready to prove Theorem
3.5
.

Proof of Theorem
3.5
.

Notice that the interpolating corrector process
(\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}},\widetilde{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}})
is constructed to follow the same dynamics as the auxiliary corrector process
(\widehat{\bm{u}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}},\widehat{\bm{v}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger},\tau^{\dagger}})
in the corrector step. Therefore, we have by data processing inequality that

\mathrm{TV}(\widehat{\pi}^{\widehat{\bm{u}}}_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}},\widetilde{\pi}^{\widetilde{{\bm{u}}}}_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}})\leq\mathrm{TV}(\widehat{\pi}^{\widehat{\bm{u}}}_{t_{n},0},\widetilde{\pi}^{\widetilde{{\bm{u}}}}_{t_{n},0})=\mathrm{TV}(\widehat{q}_{t_{n},h_{n}},\widetilde{q}_{t_{n},h_{n}}),

(C.32)

and again, since the interpolating predictor process
\widetilde{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}
is constructed to follow the same dynamics as the auxiliary predictor process
\widehat{\bm{y}}_{t_{n},n^{\dagger}h^{\dagger}}
in the predictor step, we further have by data processing inequality that

\mathrm{TV}(\widehat{q}_{t_{n},h_{n}},\widetilde{q}_{t_{n},h_{n}})\leq\mathrm{TV}(\widehat{q}_{t_{n},0},\widetilde{q}_{t_{n},0})=\mathrm{TV}(\widehat{q}_{t_{n}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}}).

(C.33)

Furthermore, applying triangle inequality, Pinsker’s inequality along with Theorem
C.17
and Theorem
C.7
proved above, we may upper bound the second term above as follows

\displaystyle\mathrm{TV}(\pi_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}},\widetilde{\pi}_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}})^{2}\lesssim D_{\mathrm{KL}}(\pi_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}}\|\widetilde{\pi}_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}})\lesssim W_{2}^{2}(\widetilde{q}_{t_{n},h_{n}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}})+\delta^{2}

(C.34)

Summarizing the above inequalities, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{TV}(\widetilde{\pi}^{\widetilde{{\bm{u}}}}_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}})^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{TV}(\widetilde{\pi}^{\widetilde{{\bm{u}}}}_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}},\pi^{*,{\bm{u}}^{*}}_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}})^{2}

(C.35)

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{TV}(\widetilde{\pi}^{\widetilde{{\bm{u}}}}_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}},\pi^{{\bm{u}}}_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}})^{2}+\mathrm{TV}(\pi^{{\bm{u}}}_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}},\pi^{*,{\bm{u}}^{*}}_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{TV}(\widetilde{\pi}_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}},\pi_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}})^{2}+\mathrm{TV}(\pi_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}},\pi^{*}_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{TV}(\widetilde{\pi}_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}},\pi_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}})^{2}+\mathrm{TV}(\pi_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}})^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim W_{2}^{2}(\widetilde{q}_{t_{n},h_{n}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}})+\delta^{2}+W_{2}^{2}(\widetilde{q}_{t_{n},h_{n}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}})

\displaystyle\lesssim de^{-K}+h_{n}^{2}\delta_{\infty}^{2}+d\epsilon^{2}h_{n}^{2}+\delta^{2},

where the second last inequality is deduced from Theorem
C.17
and Lemma
C.18
and the last inequality is derived via Theorem
C.7
. Therefore, for any
n\in[0:N-1]
, applying triangle inequality along with data processing inequality (
cf.
Theorem
A.1
) yields

\displaystyle\mathrm{TV}(\widehat{q}_{t_{n+1}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}})

\displaystyle=\mathrm{TV}(\widehat{\pi}^{\widehat{\bm{u}}}_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}})

(C.36)

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{TV}(\widehat{\pi}^{\widehat{\bm{u}}}_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}},\widetilde{\pi}^{\widetilde{{\bm{u}}}}_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}})+\mathrm{TV}(\widetilde{\pi}^{\widetilde{{\bm{u}}}}_{t_{n},T^{\dagger}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n+1}})

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{TV}(q_{t_{n}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{n}})+d^{1/2}e^{-K/2}+{h_{n}}\delta_{\infty}+d^{1/2}\epsilon{h_{n}}+\delta.

where the last inequality is derived by plugging in (
C.32
), (
C.33
) and (
C.36
). Applying Lemma
A.9
and summing the inequalities above further give us that

\displaystyle\mathrm{TV}(\widehat{q}_{t_{N}},p_{\eta})

\displaystyle=\mathrm{TV}(\widehat{q}_{t_{N}},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{t_{N}})

(C.37)

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathrm{TV}(\widehat{q}_{0},{\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\displaystyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-4.30554pt\cr$\textstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-3.01389pt\cr$\scriptstyle p$\cr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{}\mkern 4.0mu$}\cr\kern-2.15277pt\cr$\scriptscriptstyle p$\cr}}}}_{0})+\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\left(d^{1/2}e^{-\frac{K}{2}}+{h_{n}}\delta+d^{1/2}\epsilon{h_{n}}+\delta\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim d^{1/2}e^{-T/2}+Nd^{1/2}e^{-K/2}+T\delta_{\infty}+d^{1/2}\epsilon T+\delta N.

By setting the parameters

\displaystyle T={\mathcal{O}}(\log(d\delta^{-2})),\quad h=\Theta(1),\quad N={\mathcal{O}}(\log(d\delta^{-2})),

\displaystyle\epsilon=\Theta\left(d^{-1/2}\delta\log^{-1}(d^{-1/2}\delta^{-1})\right),\quad M={\mathcal{O}}(d^{1/2}\delta^{-1}\log(d^{1/2}\delta^{-1})),\quad K=\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\log(d\delta^{-2})),

and letting
\delta_{\infty}\lesssim\delta T^{-1}\lesssim\delta\log^{-1}(d\delta^{-2})
, we finally obtained the upper bound

\mathrm{TV}(\widehat{q}_{t_{N}},p_{\eta})^{2}\lesssim de^{-T}+N^{2}de^{-K}+\delta^{2}+d\epsilon^{2}T^{2}\leq\delta^{2}

as desired.
∎
